- [ ] **Step 4: Create `pipeline/atlas/methods.py`**

```python
"""Research methods (E11, D7) — a TH/EN lexicon + a sample-size reader → topic / journal / corpus aggregates.

Every article's title + abstract (TH and EN) is read internally; only aggregates are published (share of the
articles that name a method, median sample size). Text and patterns are folded the same way — Thai tone marks
dropped (atlas.thai.fold_thai), lower case — so text whose marks were lost in PDF conversion still matches.
An article may use several methods; shares are over the articles in which at least one method was found
("coverage" = that share of all articles). "Monoculture" (E11) = the share of those articles whose methods all
lie in methods.monoculture_methods (default: questionnaire + descriptive statistics only).

Step `methods` (after layout) writes work/12_methods_articles (article_id, year, topic_id, methods, sample_size),
12_methods_topics (one row per topic with ≥ methods.min_articles window articles) and 12_methods_meta.json
(lexicon version, window, corpus shares, shares by year).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from atlas.io_utils import read_table, write_table
from atlas.thai import clean_text, fold_thai


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Method:
    key: str
    th: str
    en: str
    patterns: tuple[str, ...]          # lower-case regex parts; Thai written with marks, folded at compile time


METHODS: tuple[Method, ...] = (
    Method("survey", "แบบสอบถาม/การสำรวจ", "Questionnaire / survey",
           (r"แบบสอบถาม", r"การสำรวจ", r"questionnaire", r"\bsurveys?\b")),
    Method("interview", "การสัมภาษณ์", "Interviews", (r"สัมภาษณ์", r"\binterview")),
    Method("focus_group", "การสนทนากลุ่ม", "Focus groups", (r"สนทนากลุ่ม", r"focus groups?")),
    Method("experiment", "การทดลอง/กึ่งทดลอง", "Experiment / quasi-experiment",
           (r"กึ่งทดลอง", r"การวิจัยเชิงทดลอง", r"แบบแผนการทดลอง", r"quasi[- ]?experiment",
            r"\bexperimental (?:design|study|research|group)", r"randomi[sz]ed controlled", r"\brct\b")),
    Method("qualitative", "การวิจัยเชิงคุณภาพ", "Qualitative research", (r"เชิงคุณภาพ", r"\bqualitative\b")),
    Method("mixed", "การวิจัยแบบผสานวิธี", "Mixed methods", (r"ผสานวิธี", r"วิธีวิจัยแบบผสม", r"mixed[- ]methods?")),
    Method("rnd", "การวิจัยและพัฒนา", "Research and development", (r"การวิจัยและพัฒนา", r"research and development")),
    Method("action", "การวิจัยเชิงปฏิบัติการ", "Action research",
           (r"วิจัยเชิงปฏิบัติการ", r"action research", r"\bpar\b")),
    Method("case_study", "กรณีศึกษา", "Case study", (r"กรณีศึกษา", r"case stud(?:y|ies)")),
    Method("document", "การวิเคราะห์เอกสาร/เนื้อหา", "Document / content analysis",
           (r"วิเคราะห์เอกสาร", r"วิเคราะห์เนื้อหา", r"การวิจัยเอกสาร", r"documentary research",
            r"document analysis", r"content analysis")),
    Method("review", "การทบทวนอย่างเป็นระบบ/อภิมาน", "Systematic review / meta-analysis",
           (r"ทบทวนวรรณกรรมอย่างเป็นระบบ", r"การทบทวนอย่างเป็นระบบ", r"อภิมาน", r"systematic review",
            r"meta[- ]analys", r"scoping review")),
    Method("descriptive_stats", "สถิติเชิงพรรณนา", "Descriptive statistics",
           (r"ค่าเฉลี่ย", r"ร้อยละ", r"ส่วนเบี่ยงเบนมาตรฐาน", r"สถิติเชิงพรรณนา", r"descriptive statistic",
            r"\bpercentages?\b", r"standard deviation", r"\bmeans?\b")),
    Method("inferential", "สถิติเชิงอนุมาน", "Inferential statistics",
           (r"\bt[- ]?test", r"\banova\b", r"ความแปรปรวน", r"chi[- ]square", r"ไคสแควร์", r"สหสัมพันธ์",
            r"\bcorrelation", r"ถดถอย", r"\bregression")),
    Method("sem", "สมการโครงสร้าง/องค์ประกอบ", "SEM / factor analysis",
           (r"สมการโครงสร้าง", r"structural equation", r"\bsem\b", r"องค์ประกอบเชิงยืนยัน", r"confirmatory factor",
            r"\bcfa\b", r"pls[- ]sem")),
    Method("ml", "การเรียนรู้ของเครื่อง", "Machine learning",
           (r"การเรียนรู้ของเครื่อง", r"การเรียนรู้เชิงลึก", r"โครงข่ายประสาท", r"machine learning",
            r"deep learning", r"neural networks?")),
    Method("lab", "การทดลองในห้องปฏิบัติการ", "Laboratory study", (r"ห้องปฏิบัติการ", r"in vitro", r"\blaborator")),
)
METHOD_KEYS: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(m.key for m in METHODS)
LEXICON_VERSION = hashlib.sha1(json.dumps([[m.key, list(m.patterns)] for m in METHODS],
                                          ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:8]
_RX = {m.key: re.compile("|".join(f"(?:{fold_thai(p)})" for p in m.patterns)) for m in METHODS}

_NUM = r"(\d{1,3}(?:,\d{3})+|\d+)"
_SAMPLE_PATTERNS: tuple[tuple[re.Pattern, bool], ...] = (     # (pattern, has a unit → no year check)
    (re.compile(fold_thai(r"(?:กลุ่มตัวอย่าง|ผู้ตอบแบบสอบถาม|ผู้ให้ข้อมูลสำคัญ|ผู้ให้ข้อมูล|ผู้เข้าร่วม(?:การ)?วิจัย)"
                          r"[^0-9]{0,40}?") + _NUM + fold_thai(r"\s*(?:คน|ราย|ครัวเรือน|ตัวอย่าง|แห่ง|โรงเรียน|ชุด)")),
     True),
    (re.compile(r"\bn\s*=\s*" + _NUM + r"\b"), True),
    (re.compile(_NUM + r"\s+(?:respondents|participants|informants|students|teachers|nurses|patients|farmers|"
                       r"households|employees|subjects|samples|consumers|tourists)\b"), True),
    (re.compile(r"\b(?:sample(?: size)?|respondents|participants)\b[^0-9.;]{0,30}?" + _NUM + r"\b"), False),
)
TEXT_COLUMNS = ("title_th", "title_en", "keywords_th", "keywords_en", "abstract_th", "abstract_en")
ARTICLE_COLUMNS = ["article_id", "year", "topic_id", "methods", "sample_size"]


def fold(text: str | None) -> str:
    return fold_thai(clean_text(text)).lower()


def detect_methods(text: str | None) -> list[str]:
    t = fold(text)
    return [k for k, rx in _RX.items() if rx.search(t)]


def sample_size(text: str | None, max_size: int = 1_000_000) -> int | None:
    """The first plausible sample size in the text: '...กลุ่มตัวอย่าง จำนวน 400 คน', 'n = 400', '400 respondents',
    'a sample of 400'. A number without a unit that looks like a year (1900–2100, 2400–2600 BE) is skipped."""
    t = fold(text)
    cands = []
    for rx, has_unit in _SAMPLE_PATTERNS:
        for m in rx.finditer(t):
            n = int(m.group(1).replace(",", ""))
            if not has_unit and (1900 <= n <= 2100 or 2400 <= n <= 2600):
                continue
            if 2 <= n <= max_size:
                cands.append((m.start(), n))
    return min(cands)[1] if cands else None


def article_methods(docs: pd.DataFrame, max_sample: int) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """article_id, methods ("survey; inferential" — lexicon order), sample_size (nullable Int64)."""
    cols = [docs[c].fillna("").astype(str) for c in TEXT_COLUMNS if c in docs.columns]
    texts = [" . ".join(v) for v in zip(*cols)] if cols else [""] * len(docs)
    return pd.DataFrame({
        "article_id": docs["article_id"].to_numpy(),
        "methods": ["; ".join(detect_methods(t)) for t in texts],
        "sample_size": pd.array([sample_size(t, max_sample) for t in texts], dtype="Int64"),
    })


def _sets(methods: pd.Series) -> list[set[str]]:
    return [set(x for x in str(m).split("; ") if x) for m in methods.fillna("")]


def shares_of(methods: pd.Series) -> tuple[dict[str, int], int]:
    """({method: articles naming it}, articles naming at least one method) — methods in lexicon order."""
    sets = [s for s in _sets(methods) if s]
    return {k: sum(k in s for s in sets) for k in METHOD_KEYS if any(k in s for s in sets)}, len(sets)


def monoculture_share(methods: pd.Series, mono: list[str]) -> float | None:
    sets = [s for s in _sets(methods) if s]
    return sum(s <= set(mono) for s in sets) / len(sets) if sets else None


def in_window(year: pd.Series, start: int, end: int) -> pd.Series:
    """Boolean mask of years in [start, end]; a missing year is outside."""
    return pd.to_numeric(year, errors="coerce").between(start, end).fillna(False).astype(bool)


def summarise_topics(am: pd.DataFrame, start: int, end: int, mcfg: dict) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """One row per topic with ≥ min_articles articles in [start, end]: articles, with_methods, coverage,
    counts (JSON {method: n}), sample-size n/median/q1/q3, monoculture_share, monoculture."""
    w = am[(am["topic_id"] >= 0) & in_window(am["year"], start, end)]
    rows = []
    for tid, g in w.groupby("topic_id"):
        if len(g) < int(mcfg["min_articles"]):
            continue
        counts, n_with = shares_of(g["methods"])
        ss = g["sample_size"].dropna().astype(float).to_numpy()
        mono = monoculture_share(g["methods"], mcfg["monoculture_methods"])
        rows.append({
            "topic_id": int(tid), "articles": int(len(g)), "with_methods": int(n_with),
            "coverage": n_with / len(g), "counts": json.dumps(counts),
            "ss_n": int(len(ss)), "ss_median": float(np.median(ss)) if len(ss) else np.nan,
            "ss_q1": float(np.percentile(ss, 25)) if len(ss) else np.nan,
            "ss_q3": float(np.percentile(ss, 75)) if len(ss) else np.nan,
            "monoculture_share": mono if mono is not None else np.nan,
            "monoculture": bool(mono is not None and mono >= float(mcfg["monoculture_min_share"])),
        })
    cols = ["topic_id", "articles", "with_methods", "coverage", "counts", "ss_n", "ss_median", "ss_q1", "ss_q3",
            "monoculture_share", "monoculture"]
    return pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=cols)


def by_year(am: pd.DataFrame, years: list[int]) -> dict:
    """{years, articles: [n with ≥1 method], shares: {method: [share per year]}} over every dated article."""
    out = {"years": [int(y) for y in years], "articles": [], "shares": {k: [] for k in METHOD_KEYS}}
    for y in years:
        counts, n_with = shares_of(am.loc[in_window(am["year"], y, y), "methods"])
        out["articles"].append(int(n_with))
        for k in METHOD_KEYS:
            out["shares"][k].append(round(counts.get(k, 0) / n_with, 4) if n_with else 0.0)
    out["shares"] = {k: v for k, v in out["shares"].items() if any(v)}
    return out


OUT_FILES = ("12_methods_articles.parquet", "12_methods_topics.parquet", "12_methods_meta.json",
             "12_methods_notes.parquet")


def methods(cfg: dict, llm=None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    work = Path(cfg["pipeline"]["work_dir"])
    mcfg = cfg["methods"]
    for name in OUT_FILES:                     # a re-run (or methods.enabled: false) never leaves stale tables
        (work / name).unlink(missing_ok=True)
    if not mcfg["enabled"]:
        print("[methods] methods.enabled is false — skipped (no methods module in this release)")
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=ARTICLE_COLUMNS)
    docs = read_table(work / "02_docs.parquet")
    fine = read_table(work / "04_fine_topics.parquet")[["article_id", "fine_topic"]]
    meta6 = json.loads((work / "06_meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    am = article_methods(docs, int(mcfg["max_sample_size"]))
    am = (am.merge(docs[["article_id", "year"]], on="article_id", how="left")
          .merge(fine.rename(columns={"fine_topic": "topic_id"}), on="article_id", how="left"))
    am["topic_id"] = am["topic_id"].fillna(-1).astype(int)
    am["year"] = pd.to_numeric(am["year"], errors="coerce").astype("Int64")
    am = am[ARTICLE_COLUMNS]
    write_table(am, work / "12_methods_articles.parquet")
    end = int(meta6["last_complete_year"])
    start = end - int(mcfg["window_years"]) + 1
    topics = summarise_topics(am, start, end, mcfg)
    write_table(topics, work / "12_methods_topics.parquet")
    win = am[in_window(am["year"], start, end)]
    counts, n_with = shares_of(win["methods"])
    meta = {"lexicon_version": LEXICON_VERSION, "window": [start, end], "articles": int(len(win)),
            "with_methods": int(n_with), "counts": counts,
            "monoculture_share": monoculture_share(win["methods"], mcfg["monoculture_methods"]),
            "by_year": by_year(am, [int(y) for y in meta6.get("years", [])])}
    (work / "12_methods_meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    cov = n_with / len(win) if len(win) else 0.0
    print(f"[methods] lexicon {LEXICON_VERSION}: methods named in {cov:.0%} of {len(win):,} articles {start}–{end}; "
          f"{len(topics)} topics summarised ({int(topics['monoculture'].sum()) if len(topics) else 0} monoculture)")
    return am
```

### Task 5: Opt-in LLM methods notes (topic level only)

`methods.provider: llm` (default `off`) asks `llm.tasks.methods` for a one-to-two-sentence note per large topic, from the lexicon numbers and titles only (never abstracts), grounded with the narrative checker; failures fall back to a template sentence. The job is registered so `atlas llm --dry-run` prices it and `atlas llm` runs it between `label` and `narrate`.

**Files:**
- Create: `prompts/topic_methods.th.md`
- Modify: `pipeline/atlas/methods.py`, `pipeline/atlas/config.py`, `pipeline/config.example.yaml`, `pipeline/atlas/llm_runner.py`, `pipeline/atlas/cli.py`, `pipeline/tests/test_methods.py`, `pipeline/tests/test_narrative.py`, `pipeline/tests/test_llm.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `atlas.llm_runner.LlmItem/LlmJob/run_job`, `atlas.prompts.load_prompt/prompt_version/render_prompt`, `atlas.narrative.grounding_error`, `atlas.export.fine_labels`, `atlas.lineage.topic_code`; work `05_labels`, `12_methods_topics`, `12_methods_articles`, `02_docs`.
- Produces: `atlas.methods` — `NOTES_PROMPT`, `NOTE_COLUMNS`, `MethodsNoteOut`, `method_facts(row, th, en, window, mono) -> str`, `template_note(row, th, en) -> dict`, `methods_job(cfg) -> LlmJob` (task `methods`, keys `T%06d`), `method_notes(cfg, llm) -> (DataFrame | None, stats)`; work `12_methods_notes` (topic_id, th, en, source) and `12_methods_meta.json["llm"]`; `JOBS["methods"]`; `LlmTasksCfg.methods`.

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- optional LLM notes (topic level only)
class FakeNoter:
    model_id = "fake-noter"

    def __init__(self, invent: bool = False):
        self.prompts, self.invent = [], invent
        self.model_id = "fake-inventor" if invent else "fake-noter"       # another model → no cache hit

    def chat(self, messages):
        from atlas.llm import Reply

        p = messages[0]["content"]
        self.prompts.append(p)
        out = ({"th": "ใช้แบบสอบถาม 99%", "en": "99% use questionnaires"} if self.invent else
               {"th": "บทความส่วนใหญ่ใช้แบบสอบถาม (100%)", "en": "Most articles use questionnaires (100%)"})
        return Reply(json.dumps(out, ensure_ascii=False), 0, 0, 0.0)


def _notes_work(tmp_path):
    cfg = load_config(None)
    cfg["pipeline"].update(work_dir=str(tmp_path / "w"), state_dir=str(tmp_path / "s"), release="2026Q4")
    work = tmp_path / "w"
    work.mkdir()
    cfg["methods"]["min_articles"] = 2
    docs = pd.DataFrame({"article_id": ["a", "b", "c"], "year": pd.array([2023, 2024, 2024], dtype="Int64"),
                         "title": ["เรื่อง ก", "เรื่อง ข", "เรื่อง ค"], "title_th": ["", "", ""], "title_en": ["", "", ""],
                         "abstract_th": ["ใช้แบบสอบถาม", "ใช้แบบสอบถาม", "แบบสอบถาม"], "abstract_en": ["", "", ""]})
    write_table(docs, work / "02_docs.parquet")
    write_table(pd.DataFrame({"article_id": ["a", "b", "c"], "fine_topic": [4, 4, 4]}), work / "04_fine_topics.parquet")
    write_table(pd.DataFrame({"level": ["fine"], "topic_id": [4], "name_th": ["การสำรวจความพึงพอใจ"],
                              "name_en": ["Satisfaction surveys"], "description": [""], "keywords": [""],
                              "cache_key": [""], "label_source": ["keywords"]}), work / "05_labels.parquet")
    (work / "06_meta.json").write_text(json.dumps({"last_complete_year": 2024, "years": [2023, 2024]}),
                                       encoding="utf-8")
    return cfg, work


def test_notes_are_off_by_default_and_grounded_when_on(tmp_path):
    from atlas.llm_runner import JOBS

    cfg, work = _notes_work(tmp_path)
    methods(cfg)
    assert not (work / "12_methods_notes.parquet").exists()          # provider off → no LLM, no notes
    methods(cfg, llm=FakeNoter())
    notes = read_table(work / "12_methods_notes.parquet")
    assert notes.loc[0, "source"] == "llm" and "100%" in notes.loc[0, "th"]
    meta = json.loads((work / "12_methods_meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert meta["llm"]["task"] == "methods" and meta["llm"]["items"] == 1
    noter = FakeNoter(invent=True)
    methods(cfg, llm=noter)
    notes = read_table(work / "12_methods_notes.parquet")
    assert notes.loc[0, "source"] == "template" and "99" not in notes.loc[0, "th"]
    assert "บทความเรื่อง" not in noter.prompts[0] and "เรื่อง ก" in noter.prompts[0]   # titles only, never abstracts
    assert "methods" in JOBS
```

````markdown
# Prompt: บันทึกวิธีวิจัยของหัวข้อ (topic methods note)

> **สถานะ: ใช้งานเมื่อ `methods.provider: llm` เท่านั้น (ค่าเริ่มต้น off) — ไฟล์นี้คือต้นฉบับเดียว** `pipeline/atlas/methods.py` อ่าน block ใต้ "Prompt (master)" ตอนรัน (งาน LLM `methods`)
> แก้ข้อความใน block = `prompt_version` เปลี่ยน → บันทึกถูกเขียนใหม่ในรอบถัดไป; แก้หมายเหตุนอก block ไม่มีผลต่อ cache

## บริบทการใช้

- เรียกต่อหัวข้อ (ระดับหัวข้อเท่านั้น — ไม่เคยส่งบทคัดย่อรายบทความ) เฉพาะ `methods.note_top_n` หัวข้อที่มีบทความมากที่สุดใน `work/12_methods_topics`
- inputs: `{facts}` = ตัวเลขจากคลังคำวิธีวิจัย (จำนวนบทความในช่วง, สัดส่วนที่ระบุวิธีได้, 5 วิธีที่พบบ่อย, มัธยฐานขนาดตัวอย่าง, สัดส่วน "แบบสอบถาม + สถิติพรรณนาอย่างเดียว") · `{titles}` = ชื่อบทความในหัวข้อ (บรรทัดละ 1 เรื่อง)
- **ตรวจหลังได้คำตอบ** (`narrative.grounding_error`): ตัวเลขทุกตัวต้องอยู่ใน `{facts}` และห้ามมีชื่อหัวข้ออื่น → ไม่ผ่าน = เขียนใหม่ `llm.max_regenerations` ครั้ง → ไม่ผ่านอีก = ประโยคแม่แบบจากตัวเลขชุดเดียวกัน (`source: template`)
- ผลลัพธ์ → `work/12_methods_notes` → `methods.json → topics[].note` (เว็บแสดงพร้อมป้าย "สรุปอัตโนมัติจากตัวเลขของระบบ")

## Prompt (master)

```
คุณคือที่ปรึกษาด้านระเบียบวิธีวิจัย เขียนบันทึกสั้นเรื่องวิธีวิจัยที่ใช้ในหัวข้อวิจัยหนึ่งหัวข้อ สำหรับบรรณาธิการวารสารและนักวิจัย

ข้อเท็จจริงจากระบบ (ใช้ได้เฉพาะข้อมูลนี้):
{facts}

ตัวอย่างชื่อบทความในหัวข้อนี้:
{titles}

เขียน 1–2 ประโยค เป็นภาษาไทยและภาษาอังกฤษที่ความหมายตรงกัน โดย:
1. บอกวิธีวิจัยที่พบบ่อยที่สุด พร้อมตัวเลข 1–2 ตัวจากข้อเท็จจริง
2. ถ้าข้อเท็จจริงบอกว่าบทความจำนวนมากใช้เฉพาะแบบสอบถามและสถิติพรรณนา ให้บอกตรง ๆ โดยไม่ตัดสินคุณภาพ

กติกา:
- ตัวเลขทุกตัวต้องมาจากข้อเท็จจริงข้างต้น (ปัดเศษได้) ห้ามคำนวณตัวเลขใหม่ ห้ามแปลงปีเป็น พ.ศ.
- ห้ามเอ่ยชื่อหัวข้ออื่น ห้ามเอ่ยชื่อบุคคลหรือสถาบัน ห้ามแนะนำวิธีที่ข้อมูลไม่ได้บอก

ตอบกลับเป็น JSON เท่านั้น ห้ามมีข้อความอื่น:
{
  "th": "บันทึกภาษาไทย",
  "en": "The same note in English"
}
```
````

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- optional LLM notes (topic level only)
NOTES_PROMPT = "topic_methods.th"
NOTE_COLUMNS = ["topic_id", "th", "en", "source"]
METHOD_NAMES = {m.key: m for m in METHODS}


class MethodsNoteOut(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")
    th: str
    en: str

    @field_validator("th", "en")
    @classmethod
    def _not_blank(cls, v: str) -> str:
        if not v.strip():
            raise ValueError("empty text")
        return v.strip()


def _top(counts: dict[str, int]) -> list[tuple[str, int]]:
    return sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], METHOD_KEYS.index(kv[0])))


def method_facts(row, name_th: str, name_en: str, window: tuple[int, int], mono: list[str]) -> str:
    counts = json.loads(row.counts)
    lines = [f"- หัวข้อ: {name_th} / {name_en}",
             f"- บทความช่วง {window[0]}–{window[1]}: {row.articles:,} เรื่อง; ระบุวิธีวิจัยได้ {row.with_methods:,} เรื่อง "
             f"({100 * row.coverage:.0f}%)"]
    for k, n in _top(counts)[:5]:
        m = METHOD_NAMES[k]
        lines.append(f"- {m.th} ({m.en}): {n:,} เรื่อง ({100 * n / row.with_methods:.0f}% ของบทความที่ระบุวิธีได้)")
    if row.ss_n:
        lines.append(f"- ขนาดตัวอย่าง (มัธยฐาน): {row.ss_median:,.0f} จาก {row.ss_n:,} บทความที่ระบุขนาดตัวอย่าง")
    if pd.notna(row.monoculture_share):
        names = " + ".join(METHOD_NAMES[k].th for k in mono)
        lines.append(f"- บทความที่ใช้เฉพาะ {names}: {100 * row.monoculture_share:.0f}% ของบทความที่ระบุวิธีได้")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def template_note(row, name_th: str, name_en: str) -> dict:
    top = _top(json.loads(row.counts))
    if not top:
        return {"th": f"ยังระบุวิธีวิจัยของบทความในหัวข้อ{name_th}ไม่ได้", "en": f"No method could be read for {name_en}."}
    key, n = top[0]
    m, pct = METHOD_NAMES[key], 100 * n / row.with_methods
    return {"th": f"บทความในหัวข้อนี้ที่ระบุวิธีวิจัยได้ {pct:.0f}% ใช้{m.th}",
            "en": f"{pct:.0f}% of the articles in this topic that name a method use {m.en.lower()}."}


def methods_job(cfg: dict) -> LlmJob:
    """LLM job `methods`: one item per topic among the note_top_n largest of work/12_methods_topics."""
    from atlas.export import fine_labels
    from atlas.lineage import topic_code
    from atlas.narrative import grounding_error

    work, mcfg = Path(cfg["pipeline"]["work_dir"]), cfg["methods"]
    topics = read_table(work / "12_methods_topics.parquet")
    labels = fine_labels(read_table(work / "05_labels.parquet")).set_index("topic_id")
    end = int(json.loads((work / "06_meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["last_complete_year"])
    window = (end - int(mcfg["window_years"]) + 1, end)
    template = load_prompt(NOTES_PROMPT, cfg["llm"]["prompts_dir"])
    sel = topics.sort_values(["articles", "topic_id"], ascending=[False, True]).head(int(mcfg["note_top_n"]))
    am = read_table(work / "12_methods_articles.parquet")
    titles_of = read_table(work / "02_docs.parquet").set_index("article_id")["title"].fillna("").astype(str)
    names = [str(x) for col in ("name_th", "name_en") for x in labels[col].fillna("") if str(x)]
    items = []
    for row in sel.itertuples():
        tid = int(row.topic_id)
        th = str(labels.loc[tid, "name_th"]) if tid in labels.index else topic_code(tid)
        en = str(labels.loc[tid, "name_en"]) if tid in labels.index else topic_code(tid)
        facts = method_facts(row, th, en, window, mcfg["monoculture_methods"])
        ids = am.loc[am["topic_id"] == tid, "article_id"].sort_values().head(int(cfg["narrative"]["n_titles"]))
        titles = "\n".join(f"- {titles_of.get(a, '')}" for a in ids)
        others = [n for n in names if n not in (th, en)]
        items.append(LlmItem(topic_code(tid), render_prompt(template, facts=facts, titles=titles),
                             template_note(row, th, en),
                             lambda out, f=facts + "\n" + titles, o=others: grounding_error([out["th"], out["en"]], f, o)))
    return LlmJob("methods", MethodsNoteOut, prompt_version(template), items)


def _use_llm(cfg: dict, llm) -> bool:
    return llm is not None or (cfg["methods"]["provider"] == "llm" and bool(cfg["llm"]["tasks"]["methods"]["model"]))


def method_notes(cfg: dict, llm=None) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame | None, dict]:
    """(notes, LLM stats) when methods.provider is llm (or an LLM is injected); (None, {}) otherwise."""
    if not _use_llm(cfg, llm):
        if cfg["methods"]["provider"] == "llm":
            print("[methods] methods.provider is llm but llm.tasks.methods.model is empty — no notes this run")
        return None, {}
    job = methods_job(cfg)
    if not job.items:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=NOTE_COLUMNS), {}
    results, stats = run_job(cfg, job, llm)
    rows = [{"topic_id": int(it.key[1:]), "th": results[it.key].output["th"], "en": results[it.key].output["en"],
             "source": "template" if results[it.key].source == "fallback" else "llm"} for it in job.items]
    return pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=NOTE_COLUMNS), stats
```

### Task 6: Step `journals` → work/13_*

Assembles Tasks 1–4 into the journal module: windows from `06_meta.json` (W = last `journals.window_years` complete years; R/B = the emerging windows), registry names, completeness, owners, author metrics, topic mix, peers, drift, shifts, special issues, methods per journal. A journal below `journals.min_articles` in W keeps its identity and counts but publishes no rate.

**Files:**
- Create: `pipeline/atlas/journals.py`, `pipeline/tests/p2_work.py`, `pipeline/tests/test_journals.py`
- Modify: `pipeline/atlas/pipeline.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1–4; work `01_articles`, `01_authors`, `01_references`, `02_docs`, `04_fine_topics`, `06_meta.json`, `07_emerging`, `09_edges`, `12_methods_articles` (optional).
- Produces: `atlas.journals` — `SUMMARY_COLUMNS`, `RATE_COLUMNS`, `windows(cfg, last) -> {"W","R","B": (start, end)}`, `journal_methods(ma, arts_w, eligible)`, step `journals(cfg)` → the 13_* tables listed in the module docstring (`13_journals` columns = `SUMMARY_COLUMNS`). `tests/p2_work.make_p2_work(tmp_path, release="2026Q4") -> cfg` (a hand-built work_dir reused by Tasks 7 and 9-style tests).

- [ ] **Step 1: The test work_dir** — create `pipeline/tests/p2_work.py`:

```python
"""A small hand-built work_dir for the P2 steps (journals, match).

Three journals, three topics, data to 2024:
  so02:J1  45 articles 2015–2024, topics 1 and 2, owner มหาวิทยาลัยนเรศวร (most authors), 1 Japanese co-author
  so03:J2  27 articles 2020–2024, topics 2 and 3 (topic 3 only from 2022: EMERGING, hosted by J2 alone)
  so04:J3   4 articles 2023–2024, topic 1 (low data)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

from atlas.config import load_config
from atlas.io_utils import write_table
from atlas.orgs import org_key

NU = "คณะบริหารธุรกิจ มหาวิทยาลัยนเรศวร"
CU = "คณะครุศาสตร์ จุฬาลงกรณ์มหาวิทยาลัย"
TOKYO = "Graduate School of Education, University of Tokyo, Japan"
TOPIC_NAMES = {1: ("การท่องเที่ยวเชิงชุมชน", "Community-based tourism", "การท่องเที่ยว; ชุมชน; tourism"),
               2: ("การตลาดดิจิทัล", "Digital marketing", "การตลาดออนไลน์; สื่อสังคม; digital marketing"),
               3: ("ปัญญาประดิษฐ์ในการศึกษา", "AI in education", "ปัญญาประดิษฐ์; การเรียนรู้; generative AI")}
TOPIC_TEXT = {1: "การท่องเที่ยวโดยชุมชน นักท่องเที่ยว วิสาหกิจชุมชน community tourism homestay",
              2: "การตลาดออนไลน์ สื่อสังคมออนไลน์ พฤติกรรมผู้บริโภค digital marketing social media",
              3: "ปัญญาประดิษฐ์ แชตบอต การเรียนการสอน generative AI chatbot classroom"}


def _rows():
    spec = []                                             # (journal, topic, year)
    for y in range(2015, 2025):
        spec += [("so02:J1", 1, y)] * 3 + [("so02:J1", 2, y)] * (2 if y >= 2020 else 1)
    for y in range(2020, 2025):
        spec += [("so03:J2", 2, y)] * 3 + ([("so03:J2", 3, y)] * 4 if y >= 2022 else [])
    spec += [("so04:J3", 1, 2023), ("so04:J3", 1, 2023), ("so04:J3", 1, 2024), ("so04:J3", 1, 2024)]
    return spec


def make_p2_work(tmp_path: Path, release: str = "2026Q4") -> dict:
    cfg = load_config(None)
    cfg["pipeline"].update(work_dir=str(tmp_path / "work"), output_dir=str(tmp_path / "out"),
                           state_dir=str(tmp_path / "state"), release=release)
    cfg["journals"].update(min_articles=10, drift_min_articles=5)
    cfg["journals"]["special_issue_max_hosts"] = 1
    cfg["methods"]["min_articles"] = 5
    work = Path(cfg["pipeline"]["work_dir"])
    work.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    arts, authors, refs, fine = [], [], [], []
    for i, (jk, tid, year) in enumerate(_rows()):
        host, path = jk.split(":")
        aid = f"{host}:{path}:{1000 + i}"
        method = "ใช้แบบสอบถามกับกลุ่มตัวอย่าง 400 คน วิเคราะห์ด้วยค่าเฉลี่ย" if i % 2 else "การสัมภาษณ์เชิงลึก"
        arts.append({
            "article_id": aid, "journal_name": path, "year": year, "url": f"https://{host}.tci-thaijo.org/index.php/"
            f"{path}/article/view/{1000 + i}", "doi": f"10.1/{i}" if i % 3 else "",
            "title": f"{TOPIC_NAMES[tid][0]} กรณีที่ {i}", "title_th": f"{TOPIC_NAMES[tid][0]} กรณีที่ {i}",
            "title_en": f"{TOPIC_NAMES[tid][1]} case {i}" if i % 4 else "",
            "abstract_th": f"{TOPIC_TEXT[tid]} {method}", "abstract_en": TOPIC_TEXT[tid] if i % 2 else "",
            "keywords_th": TOPIC_NAMES[tid][2], "keywords_en": TOPIC_NAMES[tid][1] if i % 5 else "",
            "has_references": True, "self_citation_only": False, "doc_text": TOPIC_TEXT[tid]})
        aff = NU if jk == "so02:J1" and i % 4 else CU
        authors.append({"article_id": aid, "position": 0, "affiliation_th": aff, "affiliation_en": "",
                        "org_key": org_key(aff, "")})
        if i == 5:
            authors.append({"article_id": aid, "position": 1, "affiliation_th": "", "affiliation_en": TOKYO,
                            "org_key": org_key("", TOKYO)})
        refs.append({"article_id": aid, "position": 0, "lang": "th", "translated": bool(i % 2)})
        fine.append({"article_id": aid, "fine_topic": tid})
    a = pd.DataFrame(arts)
    a["year"] = a["year"].astype("Int64")
    write_table(a.drop(columns=["doc_text"]), work / "01_articles.parquet")
    write_table(pd.DataFrame(authors), work / "01_authors.parquet")
    write_table(pd.DataFrame(refs), work / "01_references.parquet")
    write_table(a, work / "02_docs.parquet")
    write_table(pd.DataFrame(fine), work / "04_fine_topics.parquet")
    write_table(pd.DataFrame([{"level": "fine", "topic_id": t, "name_th": th, "name_en": en, "description": "",
                               "keywords": kw, "cache_key": "", "label_source": "keywords"}
                              for t, (th, en, kw) in TOPIC_NAMES.items()]), work / "05_labels.parquet")
    years = list(range(2015, 2025))
    totals = {str(y): int((a["year"] == y).sum()) for y in years}
    (work / "06_meta.json").write_text(json.dumps({"last_complete_year": 2024, "years": years, "totals": totals}),
                                       encoding="utf-8")
    write_table(pd.DataFrame({"topic_id": [1, 2, 3], "status": ["STABLE", "HOT", "EMERGING"],
                              "count_recent": [12, 21, 12]}), work / "07_emerging.parquet")
    write_table(pd.DataFrame({"s": [1, 2], "t": [2, 3], "w": [0.6, 0.7]}), work / "09_edges.parquet")
    return cfg
```

```python
"""Step `journals`: one table per editors' feature, aggregates only."""
import json

import pandas as pd
import pytest

from atlas.io_utils import read_table
from atlas.journals import journals
from atlas.methods import methods
from atlas.pipeline import STEPS
from tests.p2_work import make_p2_work


@pytest.fixture
def work(tmp_path):
    cfg = make_p2_work(tmp_path)
    methods(cfg)
    journals(cfg)
    return cfg, tmp_path / "work"


def test_summary_has_identity_counts_and_rates(work):
    _, w = work
    s = read_table(w / "13_journals.parquet").set_index("journal_key")
    assert list(s.index) == ["so02:J1", "so03:J2", "so04:J3"]
    j1 = s.loc["so02:J1"]
    assert j1["code"] == "so02.J1" and j1["path"] == "J1" and not j1["in_registry"] and j1["name_th"] == ""
    assert j1["articles"] == 45 and j1["articles_window"] == 25 and j1["first_year"] == 2015 and j1["active"]
    assert j1["owner_source"] == "inferred" and j1["owner_name"] == "มหาวิทยาลัยนเรศวร"
    assert 0.5 < j1["inhouse_share"] < 1 and j1["n_orgs"] == 2 and 1 < j1["effective_orgs"] < 2
    assert j1["foreign_share"] == 0                                 # the Tokyo co-author published in 2015
    assert 0 < j1["completeness"] <= 100 and j1["methods_coverage"] == 1
    j3 = s.loc["so04:J3"]
    assert j3["low_data"] and pd.isna(j3["completeness"]) and pd.isna(j3["inhouse_share"])


def test_topics_peers_drift_and_special_issue(work):
    _, w = work
    mix = read_table(w / "13_journal_topics.parquet")
    assert mix.groupby("journal_key")["share"].sum().round(9).eq(1).all()
    peers = read_table(w / "13_journal_peers.parquet")
    assert set(zip(peers["journal_key"], peers["peer_key"])) == {("so02:J1", "so03:J2"), ("so03:J2", "so02:J1")}
    drift = read_table(w / "13_journal_drift.parquet")
    j2 = drift[drift["journal_key"] == "so03:J2"].set_index("year")["distance"]
    assert pd.isna(j2[2020]) and j2[2023] > 0                       # 2020 has no reference years; 2022+ adds topic 3
    si = read_table(w / "13_special_issue.parquet")
    assert si["topic_id"].tolist() == [3] and si["hosts"].tolist() == [1]
    opp = read_table(w / "13_journal_opportunities.parquet")
    got = {(r.journal_key, r.reason) for r in opp.itertuples()}
    assert got == {("so03:J2", "in_scope"), ("so02:J1", "adjacent")}
    comp = read_table(w / "13_journal_completeness.parquet")
    assert set(comp["journal_key"]) == {"so02:J1", "so03:J2"}       # low-data journals publish no rates
    meth = read_table(w / "13_journal_methods.parquet")
    assert set(meth["method"]) >= {"survey", "interview"}
    meta = json.loads((w / "13_journals_meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert meta["windows"]["W"] == [2020, 2024] and meta["windows"]["R"] == [2022, 2024]


def test_registry_names_the_journal_and_its_owner(tmp_path):
    cfg = make_p2_work(tmp_path)
    reg = tmp_path / "journals.csv"
    reg.write_text("journal_key,name_th,tci_tier,publisher_org\nJ1,วารสาร ก,1,จุฬาลงกรณ์มหาวิทยาลัย\nnowhere,x,,\n",
                   encoding="utf-8-sig")
    cfg["journals"]["registry_path"] = str(reg)
    journals(cfg)
    s = read_table(tmp_path / "work" / "13_journals.parquet").set_index("journal_key")
    j1 = s.loc["so02:J1"]
    assert j1["in_registry"] and j1["name_th"] == "วารสาร ก" and j1["tci_tier"] == 1
    assert j1["owner_source"] == "registry" and j1["owner_name"] == "จุฬาลงกรณ์มหาวิทยาลัย" and j1["owner_matched"]
    assert 0 < j1["inhouse_share"] < 0.5
    meta = json.loads((tmp_path / "work" / "13_journals_meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert meta["registry"]["matched"] == 1 and "match no journal" in meta["registry"]["warnings"][0]


def test_step_order():
    assert STEPS.index("layout") < STEPS.index("methods") < STEPS.index("journals") < STEPS.index("export")
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `pipeline/atlas/journals.py`**

```python
"""Step `journals` (after methods): the editors' journal module → work/13_* (published by export).

Journals come from the corpus (atlas.registry.journal_key), optionally named by registry/journals.csv.
Windows (from 06_meta.json last_complete_year): W = the last journals.window_years complete years (metrics),
R / B = the emerging recent / baseline windows (topic shifts, special issues).

  13_journals              one row per journal: identity (+ registry), counts, D2 completeness score, D3 owner,
                           in-house share, institutions, effective number of institutions, foreign share,
                           affiliation coverage, methods coverage, low_data (< journals.min_articles in W → every
                           rate is null)
  13_journal_years         journal_key, year, articles (dated years ≤ last_complete_year)
  13_journal_topics        journal_key, topic_id, count, share (W, every topic — export keeps the top ones)
  13_journal_drift         journal_key, year, articles, distance (D1)
  13_journal_shifts        journal_key, topic_id, kind, share_before, share_after (B → R)
  13_journal_peers         journal_key, peer_key, similarity (D4)
  13_journal_completeness  journal_key, component, rate, n (D2)
  13_journal_methods       journal_key, method, count, share (D7, share of the articles naming a method)
  13_special_issue         topic_id, hosts, top_host_share, articles_R (D5)
  13_journal_opportunities journal_key, topic_id, reason, journal_articles, hosts (D5)
  13_journals_meta.json    windows, registry use, counts
Author rows are read here and only aggregates leave this step (PDPA).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from atlas.io_utils import read_table, write_table
from atlas.journal_metrics import article_completeness, author_metrics, completeness_table, owners
from atlas.journal_topics import (
    in_years,
    journal_opportunities,
    peer_journals,
    scope_drift,
    special_issue,
    topic_mix,
    topic_shifts,
)
from atlas.methods import shares_of
from atlas.orgs import org_display_names
from atlas.registry import journal_code, journal_keys, journal_path, load_registry, match_registry

SUMMARY_COLUMNS = [
    "journal_key", "code", "path", "name_th", "name_en", "issn", "eissn", "tci_tier", "tci_field", "publisher",
    "url", "in_registry", "articles", "articles_window", "articles_recent", "first_year", "last_year", "active",
    "low_data", "completeness", "owner_name", "owner_source", "owner_share", "owner_matched", "inhouse_share",
    "with_org", "n_orgs", "effective_orgs", "foreign_share", "affiliation_coverage", "methods_coverage",
]
RATE_COLUMNS = ["completeness", "inhouse_share", "effective_orgs", "foreign_share", "methods_coverage", "owner_share"]
AUTHOR_COLUMNS = ["article_id", "affiliation_th", "affiliation_en", "org_key"]
REF_COLUMNS = ["article_id", "lang", "translated"]


def _optional(path: Path, columns: list[str] | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame | None:
    try:
        return read_table(path)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=columns) if columns is not None else None


def windows(cfg: dict, last: int) -> dict[str, tuple[int, int]]:
    e, j = cfg["emerging"], cfg["journals"]
    r0 = last - int(e["recent_window"]) + 1
    return {"W": (last - int(j["window_years"]) + 1, last), "R": (r0, last),
            "B": (r0 - int(e["baseline_window"]), r0 - 1)}


def journal_methods(ma: pd.DataFrame | None, arts_w: pd.DataFrame, eligible: set[str]) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict]:
    """(journal_key, method, count, share) + {journal_key: coverage} for eligible journals (window articles)."""
    cols = ["journal_key", "method", "count", "share"]
    if ma is None or ma.empty:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=cols), {}
    m = ma[["article_id", "methods"]].merge(arts_w[["article_id", "journal_key"]], on="article_id")
    rows, coverage = [], {}
    for jk, g in m[m["journal_key"].isin(eligible)].groupby("journal_key"):
        counts, n_with = shares_of(g["methods"])
        coverage[jk] = n_with / len(g) if len(g) else np.nan
        rows += [{"journal_key": jk, "method": k, "count": n, "share": n / n_with} for k, n in counts.items()]
    return pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=cols), coverage


def journals(cfg: dict) -> pd.DataFrame:
    work = Path(cfg["pipeline"]["work_dir"])
    jcfg = cfg["journals"]
    meta6 = json.loads((work / "06_meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    win = windows(cfg, int(meta6["last_complete_year"]))
    W, R, B = win["W"], win["R"], win["B"]

    arts = read_table(work / "01_articles.parquet")
    arts = arts.assign(journal_key=journal_keys(arts), year=pd.to_numeric(arts["year"], errors="coerce"))
    arts = arts[arts["journal_key"] != ""].reset_index(drop=True)
    authors = _optional(work / "01_authors.parquet", AUTHOR_COLUMNS)
    refs = _optional(work / "01_references.parquet", REF_COLUMNS)

    registry, warnings = load_registry(jcfg["registry_path"])
    matched, more = match_registry(arts["journal_key"].unique(), registry)
    for w in warnings + more:
        print(f"[journals] WARNING: {w}")

    in_w = arts["year"].between(*W).fillna(False).astype(bool)
    arts_w = arts[in_w]
    counts_w = arts_w.groupby("journal_key").size()
    eligible = set(counts_w[counts_w >= int(jcfg["min_articles"])].index)

    ids_w = set(arts_w["article_id"])
    comp = article_completeness(arts_w, refs[refs["article_id"].isin(ids_w)], authors[authors["article_id"].isin(ids_w)])
    comp_long, score = completeness_table(comp, arts_w.set_index("article_id")["journal_key"],
                                          jcfg["completeness_weights"])
    reg_owner = {k: str(r.get("publisher_org") or "") for k, r in matched.items()}
    own = owners(arts_w, authors, reg_owner, float(jcfg["owner_min_share"]), org_display_names(authors))
    amet = author_metrics(arts_w, authors, own)

    docs = read_table(work / "02_docs.parquet")[["article_id"]]
    fine = read_table(work / "04_fine_topics.parquet")[["article_id", "fine_topic"]]
    jt = (docs.merge(fine, on="article_id").merge(arts[["article_id", "journal_key", "year"]], on="article_id")
          .rename(columns={"fine_topic": "topic_id"}))
    jt = jt[(jt["topic_id"] >= 0) & jt["year"].notna()].astype({"topic_id": int, "year": int})
    mix = topic_mix(jt, *W)
    peers = peer_journals(mix, eligible, int(jcfg["peers_k"]), float(jcfg["peers_min_similarity"]))
    drift = scope_drift(jt, list(range(W[0], W[1] + 1)), int(jcfg["drift_reference_years"]),
                        int(jcfg["drift_min_articles"]))
    shifts = topic_shifts(jt, B, R, int(jcfg["shift_topics"]), int(jcfg["min_articles"]))
    status = read_table(work / "07_emerging.parquet").set_index("topic_id")["status"]
    edges = _optional(work / "09_edges.parquet", ["s", "t", "w"])
    opps = special_issue(in_years(jt, *R), status, list(jcfg["special_issue_statuses"]),
                         int(jcfg["special_issue_min_host_articles"]), int(jcfg["special_issue_max_hosts"]))
    jopps = journal_opportunities(opps, mix, edges, int(jcfg["scope_topics"]), int(jcfg["special_issue_per_journal"]))
    jmeth, mcov = journal_methods(_optional(work / "12_methods_articles.parquet"), arts_w, eligible)

    dated = arts[arts["year"].notna() & (arts["year"] <= W[1])].astype({"year": int})
    years = dated.groupby(["journal_key", "year"]).size().rename("articles").reset_index()
    span = arts.dropna(subset=["year"]).groupby("journal_key")["year"].agg(["min", "max"])
    recent = arts[arts["year"].between(*R).fillna(False).astype(bool)].groupby("journal_key").size()
    total = arts.groupby("journal_key").size()
    own_i, am_i = own.set_index("journal_key"), amet.set_index("journal_key")
    rows = []
    for jk in sorted(total.index):
        reg = matched.get(jk, {})
        o = own_i.loc[jk] if jk in own_i.index else None
        a = am_i.loc[jk] if jk in am_i.index else None
        tier = reg.get("tci_tier")
        rows.append({
            "journal_key": jk, "code": journal_code(jk), "path": journal_path(jk),
            "name_th": reg.get("name_th", ""), "name_en": reg.get("name_en", ""), "issn": reg.get("issn", ""),
            "eissn": reg.get("eissn", ""), "tci_tier": int(tier) if tier is not None and pd.notna(tier) else pd.NA,
            "tci_field": reg.get("tci_field", ""), "publisher": reg.get("publisher_org", ""),
            "url": reg.get("url", ""), "in_registry": bool(reg),
            "articles": int(total[jk]), "articles_window": int(counts_w.get(jk, 0)),
            "articles_recent": int(recent.get(jk, 0)),
            "first_year": int(span.loc[jk, "min"]) if jk in span.index else pd.NA,
            "last_year": int(span.loc[jk, "max"]) if jk in span.index else pd.NA,
            "active": bool(recent.get(jk, 0) > 0), "low_data": jk not in eligible,
            "completeness": float(score.get(jk, np.nan)),
            "owner_name": str(o["owner_name"]) if o is not None else "",
            "owner_source": o["owner_source"] if o is not None and pd.notna(o["owner_source"]) else None,
            "owner_share": float(o["owner_share"]) if o is not None else np.nan,
            "owner_matched": bool(o["owner_matched"]) if o is not None else False,
            "inhouse_share": float(a["inhouse_share"]) if a is not None else np.nan,
            "with_org": int(a["with_org"]) if a is not None else 0,
            "n_orgs": int(a["n_orgs"]) if a is not None else 0,
            "effective_orgs": float(a["effective_orgs"]) if a is not None else np.nan,
            "foreign_share": float(a["foreign_share"]) if a is not None else np.nan,
            "affiliation_coverage": float(a["affiliation_coverage"]) if a is not None else np.nan,
            "methods_coverage": float(mcov.get(jk, np.nan)),
        })
    summary = pd.DataFrame(rows, columns=SUMMARY_COLUMNS)
    summary.loc[summary["low_data"], RATE_COLUMNS] = np.nan        # too few articles: no rate is published
    summary["tci_tier"] = summary["tci_tier"].astype("Int64")
    summary["first_year"] = summary["first_year"].astype("Int64")
    summary["last_year"] = summary["last_year"].astype("Int64")
    peer_rows = [{"journal_key": jk, "peer_key": p, "similarity": s} for jk, ps in peers.items() for p, s in ps]

    write_table(summary, work / "13_journals.parquet")
    write_table(years, work / "13_journal_years.parquet")
    write_table(mix, work / "13_journal_topics.parquet")
    write_table(drift, work / "13_journal_drift.parquet")
    write_table(shifts, work / "13_journal_shifts.parquet")
    write_table(pd.DataFrame(peer_rows, columns=["journal_key", "peer_key", "similarity"]),
                work / "13_journal_peers.parquet")
    write_table(comp_long[comp_long["journal_key"].isin(eligible)], work / "13_journal_completeness.parquet")
    write_table(jmeth, work / "13_journal_methods.parquet")
    write_table(opps, work / "13_special_issue.parquet")
    write_table(jopps, work / "13_journal_opportunities.parquet")
    meta = {"windows": {k: list(v) for k, v in win.items()}, "journals": int(len(summary)),
            "eligible": len(eligible), "registry": {"rows": int(len(registry)), "matched": len(matched),
                                                     "warnings": warnings + more}}
    (work / "13_journals_meta.json").write_text(json.dumps(meta, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[journals] {len(summary):,} journals ({len(eligible):,} with ≥ {jcfg['min_articles']} articles in "
          f"{W[0]}–{W[1]}; {len(matched):,} named by the registry); {len(opps)} special-issue topics")
    return summary
```

### Task 7: The match index (R3/R4) and the analyzer parity fixture

Topic vectors of character trigrams, built exactly like the browser will cut a pasted abstract. `tests/fixtures/match_parity.json` pins fold + trigram output for tricky inputs; Task 16's web test reads the same file.

**Files:**
- Create: `pipeline/atlas/matcher.py`, `pipeline/scripts/write_match_parity.py`, `pipeline/tests/fixtures/match_parity.json` (generated), `pipeline/tests/test_matcher.py`
- Modify: `pipeline/atlas/config.py`, `pipeline/config.example.yaml`, `pipeline/atlas/pipeline.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: work `02_docs`, `04_fine_topics`, `13_journals`, `13_journal_topics` (optional — no journals → no R4 list).
- Produces: `atlas.matcher` — `ANALYZER = "char_wb3"`, `FOLD_VERSION = "atlas-fold-1"`, `match_fold(text) -> str`, `char_grams(folded, n=3) -> list[str]`, `analyze(text)`, `PARITY_CASES`, `sample_members`, `topic_vectors`, `truncate`, `build_index`, `journal_mixes`, step `match(cfg) -> dict` → `14_match.json` {analyzer, fold, docs, max_terms, min_similarity, fit_topics, min_query_chars, grams, idf, topics: [{topic_id, terms, weights}], journals: [{journal_key, topics, shares}], bytes}. `atlas.config.MatchCfg` (`cfg["match"]`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — create `pipeline/tests/test_matcher.py`:

```python
"""'Match my abstract' index: analyzer parity with the web, ranking, size bound, no text published."""
import json
import math
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from atlas.journals import journals
from atlas.matcher import PARITY_CASES, analyze, char_grams, match, match_fold
from atlas.pipeline import STEPS
from tests.p2_work import TOPIC_TEXT, make_p2_work

PARITY = Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "match_parity.json"
SARA_AM = "\N{THAI CHARACTER SARA AM}"


def test_fold_matches_the_web_search_fold():
    assert match_fold("ความต้องการ") == "ความตองการ"
    assert match_fold("การท" + "\N{THAI CHARACTER NIKHAHIT}\N{THAI CHARACTER SARA AA}" + "งาน") == f"การท{SARA_AM}งาน"
    assert match_fold("  Café\tNaïve  ") == "cafe naive"
    assert match_fold("แชต" + chr(0x200B) + "บอต") == "แชตบอต"
    assert match_fold(None) == ""


def test_trigrams_follow_char_wb():
    assert char_grams("ab") == [" ab", "ab "]
    assert char_grams("a b") == [" a ", " b "]
    assert char_grams("cat") == [" ca", "cat", "at "]
    assert char_grams("") == []


def test_parity_fixture_is_up_to_date():
    cases = json.loads(PARITY.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert [c["text"] for c in cases] == list(PARITY_CASES)
    for c in cases:
        assert c["fold"] == match_fold(c["text"]) and c["grams"] == analyze(c["text"]), c["text"]


def _score(index: dict, text: str) -> list[tuple[float, int]]:
    """Reference scorer = web/src/lib/matcher.ts rankTopics: (1 + ln tf)·idf, unit length, dot product."""
    pos = {g: i for i, g in enumerate(index["grams"])}
    tf = Counter(g for g in analyze(text) if g in pos)
    q = {pos[g]: (1 + math.log(n)) * index["idf"][pos[g]] for g, n in tf.items()}
    norm = math.sqrt(sum(v * v for v in q.values())) or 1.0
    return sorted(((sum(q.get(i, 0.0) * w for i, w in zip(t["terms"], t["weights"])) / norm, t["topic_id"])
                   for t in index["topics"]), reverse=True)


@pytest.fixture
def built(tmp_path):
    cfg = make_p2_work(tmp_path)
    journals(cfg)
    return cfg, match(cfg)


def test_index_ranks_the_right_topic_and_lists_active_journals(built):
    cfg, index = built
    assert {t["topic_id"] for t in index["topics"]} == {1, 2, 3}
    for tid, text in TOPIC_TEXT.items():
        assert _score(index, text)[0][1] == tid
    t = index["topics"][0]
    assert len(t["terms"]) == len(t["weights"]) <= index["max_terms"]
    assert math.isclose(sum(w * w for w in t["weights"]), 1.0, abs_tol=1e-3)
    assert len(index["grams"]) == len(index["idf"]) and max(i for t in index["topics"] for i in t["terms"]) < len(
        index["grams"])
    assert {j["journal_key"] for j in index["journals"]} == {"so02:J1", "so03:J2"}    # J3 is low data
    saved = json.loads((Path(cfg["pipeline"]["work_dir"]) / "14_match.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert saved["bytes"] == index["bytes"] and saved["min_query_chars"] == 40


def test_the_index_is_size_bounded_and_carries_no_text(tmp_path, capsys):
    cfg = make_p2_work(tmp_path)
    cfg["match"].update(max_terms=100, min_terms=20, max_bytes=5_000)
    index = match(cfg)
    assert 20 <= index["max_terms"] < 100
    assert index["bytes"] <= 5_000 or "WARNING" in capsys.readouterr().out
    blob = json.dumps(index, ensure_ascii=False)
    assert all(len(g) <= 3 for g in index["grams"]) and TOPIC_TEXT[1][:12] not in blob


def test_disabled_match_removes_the_index(built):
    cfg, _ = built
    cfg["match"]["enabled"] = False
    assert match(cfg) == {}
    assert not (Path(cfg["pipeline"]["work_dir"]) / "14_match.json").exists()
    assert STEPS.index("journals") < STEPS.index("match") < STEPS.index("export")
```

```yaml
# ---------- P2 "วางบทคัดย่อของฉัน" + "ควรตีพิมพ์ที่ไหน" (คำนวณในเบราว์เซอร์ ไม่ส่งข้อความออกนอกเครื่อง) ----------
match:
  enabled: true
  docs_per_topic: 100         # บทความต่อหัวข้อที่ใช้สร้าง term vector (สุ่มแบบคงที่ด้วย pipeline.seed)

  min_similarity: 0.05        # หัวข้อที่ cosine < ค่านี้ไม่แสดง
  fit_topics: 5               # R4 ควรตีพิมพ์ที่ไหน: ใช้ 5 หัวข้อที่ใกล้ที่สุด × สัดส่วนหัวข้อของวารสาร

  min_query_chars: 40         # ข้อความที่วางต้องยาวอย่างน้อยเท่านี้ (หลังตัดช่องว่าง)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `pipeline/atlas/matcher.py`**

```python
"""'Match my abstract' (R3) and 'where to publish' (R4): the index the browser matches a pasted abstract against.

The browser never downloads a model and never sends the text anywhere: web/src/lib/matcher.ts folds the text,
cuts it into the same character trigrams, weights them with the published idf and ranks topics by cosine
similarity; journals are ranked by how much of their recent output falls in the closest topics.

Analyzer — identical in Python and TypeScript (tests/fixtures/match_parity.json pins both):
  fold   NFC → join a decomposed sara am → drop zero-width characters and soft hyphens → NFD → drop Latin
         combining accents (U+0300–U+036F) → drop Thai marks U+0E47–U+0E4C → lower case → collapse whitespace
         to one space, trim (= web/src/lib/search.ts foldText)
  grams  every space-separated word padded with one space on each side, then every 3-character slice
         (a padded word shorter than 3 characters is one gram) — the char_wb (3, 3) analyzer of scikit-learn
Topic vectors: up to match.docs_per_topic articles per topic (seeded sample) → gram counts summed per topic →
(1 + ln tf) · idf, idf = ln((1 + N) / (1 + df)) + 1 over the sampled articles (grams in < min_df articles are
dropped) → L2-normalised → the max_terms heaviest grams kept and renormalised. max_terms shrinks (×0.8, not
below min_terms) until the file fits match.max_bytes. Only grams, idf and weights are published — no text.

Step `match` (after journals) → work/14_match.json (internal topic ids and journal keys; export maps them).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import unicodedata
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from scipy import sparse
from sklearn.feature_extraction.text import CountVectorizer
from sklearn.preprocessing import normalize

from atlas.io_utils import read_table

ANALYZER = "char_wb3"
FOLD_VERSION = "atlas-fold-1"
N = 3
_DECOMPOSED_AM = "\N{THAI CHARACTER NIKHAHIT}\N{THAI CHARACTER SARA AA}"
_INVISIBLE = re.compile(f"[{chr(0x200B)}-{chr(0x200D)}{chr(0xFEFF)}{chr(0x00AD)}]")
_LATIN_ACCENTS = re.compile(f"[{chr(0x0300)}-{chr(0x036F)}]")
_THAI_ABOVE = re.compile(f"[{chr(0x0E47)}-{chr(0x0E4C)}]")
_SPACES = re.compile(r"\s+")
TEXT_COLUMNS = ("title_th", "title_en", "keywords_th", "keywords_en")
ABSTRACT_COLUMNS = ("abstract_th", "abstract_en")
OUT = "14_match.json"


def match_fold(text: str | None) -> str:
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", text or "").replace(_DECOMPOSED_AM, "\N{THAI CHARACTER SARA AM}")
    s = unicodedata.normalize("NFD", _INVISIBLE.sub("", s))
    s = _THAI_ABOVE.sub("", _LATIN_ACCENTS.sub("", s)).lower()
    return _SPACES.sub(" ", s).strip()


def char_grams(folded: str, n: int = N) -> list[str]:
    out: list[str] = []
    for word in folded.split(" ") if folded else []:
        w = f" {word} "
        if len(w) <= n:
            out.append(w)
        else:
            out.extend(w[i:i + n] for i in range(len(w) - n + 1))
    return out


def analyze(text: str) -> list[str]:
    return char_grams(match_fold(text))


def doc_text(row: dict, abstract_chars: int) -> str:
    parts = [str(row.get(c) or "") for c in TEXT_COLUMNS]
    parts += [str(row.get(c) or "")[:abstract_chars] for c in ABSTRACT_COLUMNS]
    return " ".join(p for p in parts if p)


def sample_members(topic_of: pd.Series, per_topic: int, seed: int) -> pd.Series:
    """article_id → topic for at most per_topic articles of each topic (seeded, independent of row order)."""
    keep = []
    for tid, ids in topic_of.groupby(topic_of).groups.items():
        ids = sorted(ids)
        if len(ids) > per_topic:
            pick = np.random.default_rng(seed + int(tid)).choice(len(ids), per_topic, replace=False)
            ids = [ids[i] for i in sorted(pick)]
        keep += ids
    return topic_of.loc[sorted(keep)]


def topic_vectors(texts: list[str], topics: np.ndarray, min_df: int) -> tuple[list[str], np.ndarray, list[int],
                                                                             sparse.csr_matrix]:
    """(grams, idf, topic ids, L2-normalised topic × gram matrix)."""
    vec = CountVectorizer(analyzer=analyze, min_df=min_df, dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        X = vec.fit_transform(texts)
    except ValueError:                             # no gram survives min_df
        return [], np.zeros(0), [], sparse.csr_matrix((0, 0))
    grams = vec.get_feature_names_out().tolist()
    df = np.asarray((X > 0).sum(axis=0)).ravel()
    idf = np.log((1 + X.shape[0]) / (1 + df)) + 1.0
    tids = sorted(set(int(t) for t in topics))
    row = {t: i for i, t in enumerate(tids)}
    T = sparse.csr_matrix((np.ones(len(topics)), ([row[int(t)] for t in topics], np.arange(len(topics)))),
                          shape=(len(tids), X.shape[0]))
    tf = (T @ X).tocsr()
    tf.data = 1.0 + np.log(tf.data)
    W = normalize(tf.multiply(idf).tocsr(), norm="l2", axis=1)
    return grams, idf, tids, W.tocsr()


def truncate(W: sparse.csr_matrix, k: int) -> list[tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]]:
    """Per row: (gram indices, weights) of the k heaviest grams, renormalised to unit length."""
    out = []
    for i in range(W.shape[0]):
        lo, hi = W.indptr[i], W.indptr[i + 1]
        idx, val = W.indices[lo:hi], W.data[lo:hi]
        order = np.lexsort((idx, -val))[:k]
        idx, val = idx[order], val[order]
        norm = float(np.sqrt((val ** 2).sum()))
        out.append((idx, val / norm if norm else val))
    return out


def build_index(grams: list[str], idf: np.ndarray, tids: list[int], W: sparse.csr_matrix,
                journals: list[dict], mcfg: dict, n_docs: int) -> dict:
    """The published index (internal ids) — max_terms shrinks until the JSON fits match.max_bytes."""
    k = int(mcfg["max_terms"])
    while True:
        rows = truncate(W, k)
        used = sorted({int(i) for idx, _ in rows for i in idx})
        pos = {g: p for p, g in enumerate(used)}
        index = {
            "analyzer": ANALYZER, "fold": FOLD_VERSION, "docs": int(n_docs), "max_terms": k,
            "min_similarity": float(mcfg["min_similarity"]), "fit_topics": int(mcfg["fit_topics"]),
            "min_query_chars": int(mcfg["min_query_chars"]),
            "grams": [grams[i] for i in used], "idf": [round(float(idf[i]), 4) for i in used],
            "topics": [{"topic_id": t, "terms": [pos[int(i)] for i in idx], "weights": [round(float(v), 4) for v in val]}
                       for t, (idx, val) in zip(tids, rows)],
            "journals": journals,
        }
        size = len(json.dumps(index, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8"))
        if size <= int(mcfg["max_bytes"]) or k <= int(mcfg["min_terms"]):
            if size > int(mcfg["max_bytes"]):
                print(f"[match] WARNING: index is {size:,} bytes > match.max_bytes even at min_terms={k}")
            index["bytes"] = size
            return index
        k = max(int(mcfg["min_terms"]), int(k * 0.8))


def journal_mixes(work: Path, mcfg: dict) -> list[dict]:
    """[{journal_key, topics, shares}] for active journals with enough articles (13_journals) — R4."""
    try:
        summary = read_table(work / "13_journals.parquet")
        mix = read_table(work / "13_journal_topics.parquet")
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return []
    ok = set(summary.loc[summary["active"].astype(bool) & ~summary["low_data"].astype(bool), "journal_key"])
    m = mix[mix["journal_key"].isin(ok) & (mix["share"] >= float(mcfg["journal_min_share"]))]
    out = []
    for jk, g in m.groupby("journal_key"):
        g = g.sort_values(["share", "topic_id"], ascending=[False, True]).head(int(mcfg["journal_topics"]))
        out.append({"journal_key": jk, "topics": [int(t) for t in g["topic_id"]],
                    "shares": [round(float(s), 4) for s in g["share"]]})
    return out


def match(cfg: dict) -> dict:
    work = Path(cfg["pipeline"]["work_dir"])
    mcfg = cfg["match"]
    (work / OUT).unlink(missing_ok=True)
    if not mcfg["enabled"]:
        print("[match] match.enabled is false — skipped (no match index in this release)")
        return {}
    docs = read_table(work / "02_docs.parquet")
    fine = read_table(work / "04_fine_topics.parquet")[["article_id", "fine_topic"]]
    d = docs.merge(fine, on="article_id")
    d = d[d["fine_topic"] >= 0]
    topic_of = d.set_index("article_id")["fine_topic"].astype(int)
    picked = sample_members(topic_of, int(mcfg["docs_per_topic"]), int(cfg["pipeline"]["seed"]))
    rows = d.set_index("article_id").loc[picked.index]
    texts = [doc_text(r, int(mcfg["abstract_chars"])) for r in rows.to_dict("records")]
    grams, idf, tids, W = topic_vectors(texts, picked.to_numpy(), int(mcfg["min_df"]))
    index = build_index(grams, idf, tids, W, journal_mixes(work, mcfg), mcfg, len(texts))
    (work / OUT).write_text(json.dumps(index, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[match] {len(index['topics'])} topic vectors × ≤ {index['max_terms']} trigrams "
          f"({len(index['grams']):,} distinct), {len(index['journals'])} journals, {index['bytes'] / 1e6:.2f} MB")
    return index


# Inputs whose fold + grams are pinned in tests/fixtures/match_parity.json (scripts/write_match_parity.py) and
# checked by both test suites — Thai tone marks, a decomposed sara am, accents, zero-width space, odd spacing.
PARITY_CASES: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "ความต้องการของผู้สูงอายุในชุมชน",
    "การท" + _DECOMPOSED_AM + "งานเป็นทีม",
    "Café   Naïve  AI-based  Learning",
    "แชต" + chr(0x200B) + "บอต" + chr(0x00AD) + "ในห้องเรียน",
    "line1\nline2\tx  ",
    "การตลาดดิจิทัล (Digital Marketing) 2024",
    "a b ab",
    "",
)
```

```python
"""Write tests/fixtures/match_parity.json — the fold + trigram output of atlas.matcher for PARITY_CASES.

The web test (web/src/lib/matcher.test.ts) reads the same file, so the Python and TypeScript analyzers cannot
drift apart. Run from pipeline/:  uv run python scripts/write_match_parity.py
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

from atlas.matcher import PARITY_CASES, analyze, match_fold  # noqa: E402

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "tests" / "fixtures" / "match_parity.json"


def parity_cases() -> list[dict]:
    return [{"text": t, "fold": match_fold(t), "grams": analyze(t)} for t in PARITY_CASES]


if __name__ == "__main__":
    OUT.write_text(json.dumps(parity_cases(), ensure_ascii=False, indent=1) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"wrote {OUT} ({len(PARITY_CASES)} cases)")
```

### Task 8: Quarter diff (E5) and release snapshots

A compact snapshot of each release is written to `state_dir` at export (Task 9 wires it); the diff of two snapshots lists new / split / merged topics, retired topics, status changes, movers by recent share, KPI changes and newly covered journals.

**Files:**
- Create: `pipeline/atlas/diff.py`, `pipeline/tests/test_diff.py`
- Modify: `pipeline/atlas/config.py`, `pipeline/config.example.yaml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `atlas.state.release_dir`.
- Produces: `atlas.diff` — `SNAPSHOT`, `KPI_KEYS`, `make_snapshot(release, topics: list[Topic dict], kpis: dict, journal_ids | None) -> dict`, `write_snapshot(cfg, release, snap) -> Path`, `load_snapshot(cfg, release) -> (dict | None, "state" | "output" | "")`, `quarter_diff(cur, prev, lineage, movers, min_share_delta) -> dict` (keys = `DiffFile` fields except `schema_version` and `previous_source`). `atlas.config.ChangesCfg` (`cfg["changes"]`).

```python
"""Quarter diff: snapshots in state_dir, fallback to the previous release folder, what changed."""
import json

import pytest

from atlas.config import load_config
from atlas.diff import load_snapshot, make_snapshot, quarter_diff, write_snapshot


def _topic(tid, status, size, share, th="ก"):
    return {"id": tid, "status": status, "size": size, "score": 50.0, "th": th, "en": tid,
            "metrics": {"share_recent": share}}


PREV = make_snapshot("2026Q3", [_topic("T000001", "STABLE", 100, 0.010), _topic("T000002", "HOT", 300, 0.030),
                                _topic("T000003", "DECLINING", 50, 0.004, th="เก่า")],
                     {"articles": 1000, "topics": 3, "journals": 2}, ["so02.A", "so02.B"])
CUR = make_snapshot("2026Q4", [_topic("T000001", "EMERGING", 140, 0.016), _topic("T000002", "HOT", 310, 0.0302),
                               _topic("T000004", "LOW_DATA", 20, 0.001), _topic("T000005", "STABLE", 60, 0.005)],
                    {"articles": 1100, "topics": 4, "journals": 3}, ["so02.A", "so02.B", "so02.C"])


def test_quarter_diff_lists_new_retired_changed_and_movers():
    lineage = {"T000004": {"event": "split", "parents": ["T000003"]}, "T000005": {"event": "new", "parents": []}}
    d = quarter_diff(CUR, PREV, lineage, movers=10, min_share_delta=0.0005)
    assert d["release"] == "2026Q4" and d["previous_release"] == "2026Q3"
    assert d["new_topics"] == [{"topic": "T000004", "event": "split", "parents": ["T000003"]},
                               {"topic": "T000005", "event": "new", "parents": []}]
    assert d["retired"] == [{"topic": "T000003", "th": "เก่า", "en": "T000003"}]
    assert d["status_changes"] == [{"topic": "T000001", "previous": "STABLE", "current": "EMERGING"}]
    assert [m["topic"] for m in d["movers"]] == ["T000001"]          # T000002 moved 0.0002 < 0.0005
    assert {k["key"]: (k["previous"], k["current"]) for k in d["kpis"]}["articles"] == (1000, 1100)
    assert d["journals_compared"] and d["new_journals"] == ["so02.C"]
    no_j = quarter_diff(CUR, {**PREV, "journals": None}, {}, 10, 0.0)
    assert not no_j["journals_compared"] and no_j["new_journals"] == []
    assert len(quarter_diff(CUR, PREV, {}, movers=1, min_share_delta=0.0)["movers"]) == 1

                                        encoding="utf-8")
    (folder / "overview.json").write_text(json.dumps({"kpis": {"articles": 10, "topics": 1}}), encoding="utf-8")
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `pipeline/atlas/diff.py`**

```python
"""Quarter diff (E5 "what changed since the previous release") → diff.json and page /changes.

Every export writes a compact snapshot of its release to state_dir/releases/<release>/snapshot.json
(topics: id → status, size, share_recent, score, names; KPIs; journal ids) — state_dir is the persistent
store, so the next quarter can compare even when output/ was cleaned. A previous release exported before P2 has
no snapshot: the diff then reads output/data/<previous>/topics.json + overview.json (+ journals.json when
present). Neither available → no diff.json (the web explains that /changes needs a previous release).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

from atlas.state import release_dir

SNAPSHOT = "snapshot.json"
KPI_KEYS = ("articles", "assigned_articles", "topics", "journals", "institutions")
STATUS_ORDER = ("EMERGING", "WATCH", "HOT", "DECLINING", "STABLE", "LOW_DATA")


def make_snapshot(release: str, topics: list[dict], kpis: dict, journal_ids: list[str] | None) -> dict:
    """topics: contract Topic dicts (id, status, size, score, th, en, metrics.share_recent)."""
    return {"release": release,
            "topics": {t["id"]: {"status": t["status"], "size": int(t["size"]), "score": float(t.get("score") or 0.0),
                                 "share_recent": float(t["metrics"]["share_recent"]), "th": t.get("th", ""),
                                 "en": t.get("en", "")} for t in topics},
            "kpis": {k: kpis[k] for k in KPI_KEYS if k in kpis},
            "journals": sorted(journal_ids) if journal_ids is not None else None}


def write_snapshot(cfg: dict, release: str, snap: dict) -> Path:
    d = release_dir(cfg, release)
    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    p = d / SNAPSHOT
    p.write_text(json.dumps(snap, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True), encoding="utf-8")
    return p


def _load(path: Path) -> dict | None:
    try:
        return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (FileNotFoundError, ValueError):
        return None


def load_snapshot(cfg: dict, release: str) -> tuple[dict | None, str]:
    """(snapshot, source "state" | "output") of a release, or (None, "")."""
    snap = _load(release_dir(cfg, release) / SNAPSHOT)
    if snap is not None:
        return snap, "state"
    folder = Path(cfg["pipeline"]["output_dir"]) / "data" / release
    topics, overview = _load(folder / "topics.json"), _load(folder / "overview.json")
    try:
        rows = [t for t in topics["topics"] if isinstance(t, dict) and {"id", "status", "size", "metrics"} <= set(t)]
        kpis = overview["kpis"]
    except (TypeError, KeyError):
        return None, ""
    journals = _load(folder / "journals.json")
    ids = [j["id"] for j in journals.get("journals", [])] if isinstance(journals, dict) else None
    return make_snapshot(release, rows, kpis, ids), "output"


def quarter_diff(cur: dict, prev: dict, lineage: dict[str, dict], movers: int, min_share_delta: float) -> dict:
    """What changed from `prev` to `cur` (snapshots). lineage: current topic id → {event, parents}."""
    ct, pt = cur["topics"], prev["topics"]
    common = sorted(set(ct) & set(pt))
    new = []
    for tid in sorted(set(ct) - set(pt)):
        lin = lineage.get(tid, {})
        event = lin.get("event") if lin.get("event") in ("split", "merge") else "new"
        new.append({"topic": tid, "event": event, "parents": list(lin.get("parents", []))})
    retired = [{"topic": tid, "th": pt[tid].get("th", ""), "en": pt[tid].get("en", "")}
               for tid in sorted(set(pt) - set(ct))]
    order = {s: i for i, s in enumerate(STATUS_ORDER)}
    changes = sorted(({"topic": t, "previous": pt[t]["status"], "current": ct[t]["status"]}
                      for t in common if pt[t]["status"] != ct[t]["status"]),
                     key=lambda r: (order.get(r["current"], 99), r["topic"]))
    moved = [{"topic": t, "share_previous": round(pt[t]["share_recent"], 6), "share_current": round(ct[t]["share_recent"], 6)}
             for t in common if abs(ct[t]["share_recent"] - pt[t]["share_recent"]) >= min_share_delta]
    moved.sort(key=lambda r: (-abs(r["share_current"] - r["share_previous"]), r["topic"]))
    kpis = [{"key": k, "previous": float(prev["kpis"][k]), "current": float(cur["kpis"][k])}
            for k in KPI_KEYS if k in cur["kpis"] and k in prev.get("kpis", {})]
    compared = cur.get("journals") is not None and prev.get("journals") is not None
    return {"release": cur["release"], "previous_release": prev["release"], "kpis": kpis, "new_topics": new,
            "retired": retired, "status_changes": changes, "movers": moved[:movers],
            "journals_compared": compared,
            "new_journals": sorted(set(cur["journals"]) - set(prev["journals"])) if compared else []}
```

### Task 9: Contract 2.1 and the export of the editors' files

New pydantic models (all module files optional), `CountItem.id` so topic pages can link journals, the editors' files built by `export_editors.py`, the diff from the previous snapshot, and the snapshot written after every export. Regenerate the JSON Schema and the TypeScript types.

**Files:**
- Create: `pipeline/atlas/export_editors.py`, `pipeline/tests/test_contract_p2.py`
- Modify: `pipeline/atlas/schema/contract.py`, `pipeline/atlas/export_v2.py`, `pipeline/tests/test_release.py`, `pipeline/tests/test_contract_v2.py`, `pipeline/tests/test_schema.py`
- Regenerate: `web/src/data/contract.schema.json`, `web/src/data/contract.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 4–8 checkpoints; `atlas.diff`; `atlas.registry.journal_keys/journal_path`; `atlas.methods.METHODS`.
- Produces: contract `SCHEMA_VERSION = "2.1.0"`, `JOURNAL_ID`, models `YearWindow`, `JournalSummary`, `SpecialIssueTopic`, `JournalsFile` (incl. `special_issue_statuses`, `special_issue_max_hosts`, `special_issue_min_host_articles`, `drift_reference_years`, `inhouse_flag_share`), `JournalYear`, `TopicShare`, `DriftPoint`, `TopicShift`, `Peer`, `CompletenessItem`, `MethodShare`, `JournalOpportunity`, `JournalDetail`, `MethodInfo`, `MethodYears`, `TopicMethods`, `MethodsFile`, `KpiChange`, `NewTopic`, `RetiredTopic`, `StatusChange`, `Mover`, `DiffFile`, `MatchTopic`, `MatchJournal`, `MatchIndex`, `P2_FILE_MODELS`; `model_for("journals/<id>.json") -> JournalDetail`. `atlas.export_editors.journal_files(cfg, work, code, topic_group) -> (files, {key: id}, {key: name})`, `methods_file(cfg, work, code)`, `match_file(work, code, jid)`. `atlas.export_v2.release_snapshot(release, files)`, `release_diff(cfg, release, prev, files)`. Published: `journals.json`, `journals/<id>.json`, `methods.json`, `match.json`, `diff.json`; `manifest.modules` += `journals`, `journal_detail`, `methods`, `match`, `diff` (each only when present); `manifest.llm` lists the `methods` task when it ran.

```python
"""Contract 2.1: the editors' files validate strictly and appear in the JSON Schema."""
import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from atlas.schema.contract import (
    SCHEMA_VERSION,
    CountItem,
    DiffFile,
    JournalDetail,
    JournalSummary,
    MatchIndex,
    MethodYears,
    contract_json_schema,
    model_for,
)

SUMMARY = {"id": "so02.BECJournal", "key": "so02:BECJournal", "path": "BECJournal", "articles": 40,
           "articles_window": 25, "active": True, "low_data": False}


def test_schema_version_and_definitions():
    assert SCHEMA_VERSION.startswith("2.") and SCHEMA_VERSION != "2.0.0"
    schema = contract_json_schema()
    assert {"journals", "journal_detail", "methods", "diff", "match"} <= set(schema["properties"])
    assert schema["definitions"]["CountItem"]["properties"]["id"]["default"] is None
    assert model_for("journals/so02.BECJournal.json") is JournalDetail
    assert model_for("journals.json").__name__ == "JournalsFile"


def test_journal_fields_from_the_registry_are_optional():
    j = JournalSummary.model_validate(SUMMARY)
    assert j.name_th == "" and j.tci_tier is None and j.owner_source is None and j.completeness is None
    for bad in ({"id": "so02:BECJournal"}, {"tci_tier": 4}, {"completeness": 101}, {"owner_source": "guess"}):
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
            JournalSummary.model_validate({**SUMMARY, **bad})
    assert CountItem(key="BECJournal", count=3).id is None


def test_match_index_must_be_aligned():
    base = {"analyzer": "char_wb3", "fold": "atlas-fold-1", "docs": 10, "max_terms": 2, "min_similarity": 0.05,
            "fit_topics": 5, "min_query_chars": 40, "grams": [" ab", "ab "], "idf": [1.0, 1.2],
            "topics": [{"id": "T000001", "terms": [0, 1], "weights": [0.6, 0.8]}],
            "journals": [{"id": "so02.A", "topics": ["T000001"], "shares": [0.5]}]}
    assert MatchIndex.model_validate(base).topics[0].id == "T000001"
    for bad in ({"idf": [1.0]}, {"topics": [{"id": "T000001", "terms": [0, 2], "weights": [0.6, 0.8]}]},
                {"journals": [{"id": "so02.A", "topics": ["T000001"], "shares": []}]},
                {"analyzer": "word"}, {"idf": [1.0, float("nan")]}):
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
            MatchIndex.model_validate({**base, **bad})


def test_method_years_and_diff_are_strict():
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        MethodYears(years=[2023, 2024], articles=[1, 2], shares={"survey": [0.5]})
    d = {"release": "2026Q4", "previous_release": "2026Q3", "previous_source": "state", "kpis": [],
         "new_topics": [{"topic": "T000004", "event": "split", "parents": ["T000003"]}], "retired": [],
         "status_changes": [{"topic": "T000001", "previous": "STABLE", "current": "EMERGING"}], "movers": [],
         "journals_compared": True}
    assert DiffFile.model_validate(d).new_journals == []
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        DiffFile.model_validate({**d, "status_changes": [{"topic": "T000001", "previous": "OLD", "current": "HOT"}]})
```

Append to `pipeline/tests/test_release.py`:

```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- P2: editors' modules (contract 2.1)
def test_editor_modules_are_published_and_valid(two_releases):
    root, _ = two_releases
    rel = root / "out" / "data" / "2026Q2"
    man = Manifest.model_validate_json((rel / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert man.schema_version == "2.1.0"
    assert {"journals", "journal_detail", "methods", "match", "diff"} <= set(man.modules)
    paths = {f.path for f in man.files}
    assert {"journals.json", "journals/so02.BECJournal.json", "methods.json", "match.json", "diff.json"} <= paths
    for p in paths:
        model_for(p).model_validate_json((rel / p).read_bytes())
    jf = json.loads((rel / "journals.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    j = jf["journals"][0]
    assert j["id"] == "so02.BECJournal" and j["path"] == "BECJournal" and j["name_th"] == ""
    assert j["low_data"] is True and j["inhouse_share"] is None      # 11 articles < journals.min_articles
    topics = json.loads((rel / "topics.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["topics"]
    detail = json.loads((rel / f"topics/{topics[0]['id']}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert detail["journals"][0]["id"] == "so02.BECJournal" and detail["journals"][0]["key"] == "BECJournal"
    diff = json.loads((rel / "diff.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert diff["previous_release"] == "2026Q1" and diff["previous_source"] == "state"
    assert "diff" not in json.loads((root / "out" / "data" / "2026Q1" / "manifest.json").read_text(
        encoding="utf-8"))["modules"]                                 # the first release has nothing to compare
    assert (root / "state" / "releases" / "2026Q2" / "snapshot.json").exists()


def test_registry_names_journals_in_the_release(two_releases, tmp_path):
    cfg = _workspace(two_releases, tmp_path, "2026Q2")
    reg = tmp_path / "journals.csv"
    reg.write_text("journal_key,name_th,tci_tier,publisher_org\nBECJournal,วารสารทดสอบ,2,มหาวิทยาลัยนเรศวร\n",
                   encoding="utf-8")
    cfg = {**cfg, "journals": {**cfg["journals"], "registry_path": str(reg)}}   # never edit the shared fixture
    run(cfg, ["journals", "export"])
    rel = Path(cfg["pipeline"]["output_dir"]) / "data" / "2026Q2"
    j = json.loads((rel / "journals.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["journals"][0]
    assert j["name_th"] == "วารสารทดสอบ" and j["tci_tier"] == 2 and j["owner_source"] == "registry"
    topics = json.loads((rel / "topics.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["topics"]
    detail = json.loads((rel / f"topics/{topics[0]['id']}.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert detail["journals"][0]["key"] == "วารสารทดสอบ"
```
