```python
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- P2 editors' modules (schema 2.1)
JOURNAL_ID = r"^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,80}$"
Completeness = Literal["abstract_en", "title_en", "keywords_en", "doi", "references", "refs_romanized", "affiliations"]


class YearWindow(_Model):
    start: int
    end: int


class JournalSummary(_Model):
    id: str = Field(pattern=JOURNAL_ID, description="journals/<id>.json; stable across releases")
    key: str = Field(description="corpus key <server>:<OJS path> (or name:<normalised name>)")
    path: str = Field(description="OJS path — shown when the registry gives no name")
    name_th: str = ""
    name_en: str = ""
    issn: str = ""
    eissn: str = ""
    tci_tier: int | None = Field(None, ge=1, le=3)
    tci_field: str = ""
    publisher: str = ""
    url: str = ""
    in_registry: bool = False
    articles: int
    articles_window: int
    articles_recent: int = 0
    first_year: int | None = None
    last_year: int | None = None
    active: bool = Field(description="published in the recent window R")
    low_data: bool = Field(description="fewer than min_articles in the window: every rate is null")
    group: str | None = Field(None, description="map group holding most of its window articles")
    completeness: float | None = Field(None, ge=0, le=100, description="D2 metadata completeness 0–100 (not the "
                                                                       "official TCI assessment)")
    owner: str = Field("", description="owner institution as written (registry) or its most frequent written name")
    owner_source: Literal["registry", "inferred"] | None = None
    owner_matched: bool = Field(False, description="the owner was found among the authors' organisations")
    inhouse_share: float | None = None
    n_orgs: int = 0
    effective_orgs: float | None = None
    foreign_share: float | None = None
    affiliation_coverage: float | None = None
    methods_coverage: float | None = None


class SpecialIssueTopic(_Model):
    topic: str = Field(pattern=TOPIC_ID)
    hosts: int
    top_host_share: float
    articles_recent: int


class JournalsFile(_Model):
    schema_version: str = SCHEMA_VERSION
    window: YearWindow
    recent: YearWindow
    baseline: YearWindow
    min_articles: int
    completeness_weights: dict[str, float]
    inhouse_flag_share: float | None = None
    special_issue_statuses: list[Status] = Field(default_factory=list)
    special_issue_max_hosts: int = 0
    special_issue_min_host_articles: int = 0
    drift_reference_years: int = 0
    journals: list[JournalSummary]
    special_issue: list[SpecialIssueTopic] = Field(default_factory=list)


class JournalYear(_Model):
    year: int
    articles: int


class TopicShare(_Model):
    topic: str = Field(pattern=TOPIC_ID)
    count: int
    share: float


class DriftPoint(_Model):
    year: int
    articles: int
    distance: float | None = Field(description="Jensen–Shannon distance to the reference years; null = too few")


class TopicShift(_Model):
    topic: str = Field(pattern=TOPIC_ID)
    share_before: float
    share_after: float


class Peer(_Model):
    id: str = Field(pattern=JOURNAL_ID)
    similarity: float


class CompletenessItem(_Model):
    key: Completeness
    rate: float | None
    n: int


class MethodShare(_Model):
    method: str
    count: int
    share: float


class JournalOpportunity(_Model):
    topic: str = Field(pattern=TOPIC_ID)
    reason: Literal["in_scope", "adjacent"]
    journal_articles: int
    hosts: int


class JournalDetail(_Model):
    schema_version: str = SCHEMA_VERSION
    id: str = Field(pattern=JOURNAL_ID)
    years: list[JournalYear] = Field(default_factory=list)
    topics: list[TopicShare] = Field(default_factory=list)
    drift: list[DriftPoint] = Field(default_factory=list)
    gained: list[TopicShift] = Field(default_factory=list)
    lost: list[TopicShift] = Field(default_factory=list)
    peers: list[Peer] = Field(default_factory=list)
    completeness: list[CompletenessItem] = Field(default_factory=list)
    methods: list[MethodShare] = Field(default_factory=list)
    opportunities: list[JournalOpportunity] = Field(default_factory=list)


class MethodInfo(_Model):
    key: str
    th: str
    en: str


class MethodYears(_Model):
    years: list[int]
    articles: list[int]
    shares: dict[str, list[float]]

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _aligned(self):
        n = len(self.years)
        if len(self.articles) != n or any(len(v) != n for v in self.shares.values()):
            raise ValueError("every method series must have one value per year")
        return self


class TopicMethods(_Model):
    topic: str = Field(pattern=TOPIC_ID)
    articles: int
    with_methods: int
    coverage: float
    methods: list[MethodShare]
    sample_size_n: int = 0
    sample_size_median: float | None = None
    sample_size_q1: float | None = None
    sample_size_q3: float | None = None
    monoculture_share: float | None = None
    monoculture: bool = False
    note: Narrative | None = None


class MethodsFile(_Model):
    schema_version: str = SCHEMA_VERSION
    lexicon_version: str
    window: YearWindow
    articles: int
    with_methods: int
    monoculture_methods: list[str]
    monoculture_min_share: float
    monoculture_share: float | None = None
    min_articles: int
    methods: list[MethodInfo]
    corpus: list[MethodShare]
    by_year: MethodYears
    topics: list[TopicMethods]


class KpiChange(_Model):
    key: str
    previous: float
    current: float


class NewTopic(_Model):
    topic: str = Field(pattern=TOPIC_ID)
    event: Literal["new", "split", "merge"]
    parents: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)


class RetiredTopic(_Model):
    topic: str = Field(pattern=TOPIC_ID)
    th: str = ""
    en: str = ""


class StatusChange(_Model):
    topic: str = Field(pattern=TOPIC_ID)
    previous: Status
    current: Status


class Mover(_Model):
    topic: str = Field(pattern=TOPIC_ID)
    share_previous: float
    share_current: float


class DiffFile(_Model):
    schema_version: str = SCHEMA_VERSION
    release: str
    previous_release: str
    previous_source: Literal["state", "output"]
    kpis: list[KpiChange]
    new_topics: list[NewTopic]
    retired: list[RetiredTopic]
    status_changes: list[StatusChange]
    movers: list[Mover]
    journals_compared: bool
    new_journals: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)


class MatchTopic(_Model):
    id: str = Field(pattern=TOPIC_ID)
    terms: list[int]
    weights: list[float]


class MatchJournal(_Model):
    id: str = Field(pattern=JOURNAL_ID)
    topics: list[str]
    shares: list[float]


class MatchIndex(_Model):
    schema_version: str = SCHEMA_VERSION
    analyzer: Literal["char_wb3"]
    fold: Literal["atlas-fold-1"]
    docs: int
    max_terms: int
    min_similarity: float
    fit_topics: int
    min_query_chars: int
    grams: list[str]
    idf: list[float]
    topics: list[MatchTopic]
    journals: list[MatchJournal] = Field(default_factory=list)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _aligned(self):
        if len(self.grams) != len(self.idf):
            raise ValueError("grams and idf differ in length")
        n = len(self.grams)
        for t in self.topics:
            if len(t.terms) != len(t.weights) or any(not 0 <= i < n for i in t.terms):
                raise ValueError(f"match topic {t.id}: terms/weights misaligned or out of range")
        for j in self.journals:
            if len(j.topics) != len(j.shares):
                raise ValueError(f"match journal {j.id}: topics/shares misaligned")
        return self


P2_FILE_MODELS: dict[str, type[_Model]] = {"journals.json": JournalsFile, "methods.json": MethodsFile,
                                           "diff.json": DiffFile, "match.json": MatchIndex}
```

```python
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `pipeline/atlas/export_editors.py`**

```python
"""Contract 2.1 files of the editors' release (P2), built from work/12_* (methods), 13_* (journals), 14_* (match)
and the previous release's snapshot (diff). Called by atlas.export_v2.build_release; each module is optional —
a missing checkpoint simply leaves its files (and its manifest.modules entry) out.

journals.json            every journal: identity (+ registry), counts, D2 completeness, D3 owner / in-house /
                         institutions / foreign share, group; special-issue topics (D5)
journals/<id>.json       years, topic mix (top journals.top_topics), scope drift (D1), gained/lost topics, peers
                         (D4), completeness by element (D2), methods mix (D7), special-issue opportunities (D5)
methods.json             lexicon, corpus shares, shares by year, per-topic shares + sample size + monoculture (E11)
match.json               the 'match my abstract' index (R3/R4) with published ids
Only aggregates are published; no abstract, no person.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

from atlas.io_utils import read_table
from atlas.methods import METHODS
from atlas.schema import contract as C


def _f(v, nd: int = 4) -> float | None:
    try:
        x = float(v)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return round(x, nd) if math.isfinite(x) else None


def _opt(path: Path) -> pd.DataFrame | None:
    try:
        return read_table(path)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return None


def _window(pair) -> C.YearWindow:
    return C.YearWindow(start=int(pair[0]), end=int(pair[1]))


def _int_or_none(v) -> int | None:
    return int(v) if v is not None and pd.notna(v) else None


def journal_files(cfg: dict, work: Path, code: dict[int, str], topic_group: dict[int, str]
                  ) -> tuple[dict[str, C._Model], dict[str, str], dict[str, str]]:
    """({path: model}, {journal_key: id}, {journal_key: display name}) — empty when the journals step did not run."""
    summary = _opt(work / "13_journals.parquet")
    if summary is None:
        return {}, {}, {}
    jcfg = cfg["journals"]
    meta = json.loads((work / "13_journals_meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    mix = read_table(work / "13_journal_topics.parquet")
    mix = mix[mix["topic_id"].astype(int).isin(list(code))]
    jid = dict(zip(summary["journal_key"], summary["code"]))
    names = {r.journal_key: (r.name_th or r.name_en or r.path) for r in summary.itertuples()}
    by = {name: t.groupby("journal_key") for name, t in {
        "years": read_table(work / "13_journal_years.parquet"), "mix": mix,
        "drift": read_table(work / "13_journal_drift.parquet"), "shifts": read_table(work / "13_journal_shifts.parquet"),
        "peers": read_table(work / "13_journal_peers.parquet"),
        "comp": read_table(work / "13_journal_completeness.parquet"),
        "methods": read_table(work / "13_journal_methods.parquet"),
        "opps": read_table(work / "13_journal_opportunities.parquet")}.items()}
    groups = {k: dict(tuple(g)) for k, g in by.items()}
    group_of = {}
    for jk, g in groups["mix"].items():
        per = g.assign(group=g["topic_id"].astype(int).map(topic_group)).groupby("group")["count"].sum()
        group_of[jk] = str(per.sort_values(ascending=False).index[0]) if len(per) else None

    rows, files = [], {}
    for r in summary.itertuples():
        jk = r.journal_key
        rows.append(C.JournalSummary(
            id=r.code, key=jk, path=r.path, name_th=r.name_th or "", name_en=r.name_en or "", issn=r.issn or "",
            eissn=r.eissn or "", tci_tier=_int_or_none(r.tci_tier), tci_field=r.tci_field or "",
            publisher=r.publisher or "", url=r.url or "", in_registry=bool(r.in_registry), articles=int(r.articles),
            articles_window=int(r.articles_window), articles_recent=int(r.articles_recent),
            first_year=_int_or_none(r.first_year), last_year=_int_or_none(r.last_year), active=bool(r.active),
            low_data=bool(r.low_data), group=group_of.get(jk), completeness=_f(r.completeness, 1),
            owner=r.owner_name or "", owner_source=r.owner_source if r.owner_source in ("registry", "inferred") else None,
            owner_matched=bool(r.owner_matched), inhouse_share=_f(r.inhouse_share), n_orgs=int(r.n_orgs),
            effective_orgs=_f(r.effective_orgs, 2), foreign_share=_f(r.foreign_share),
            affiliation_coverage=_f(r.affiliation_coverage), methods_coverage=_f(r.methods_coverage)))
        g = {k: v.get(jk) for k, v in groups.items()}
        topics = g["mix"].head(int(jcfg["top_topics"])) if g["mix"] is not None else None
        shifts = g["shifts"]
        files[f"journals/{r.code}.json"] = C.JournalDetail(
            id=r.code,
            years=[C.JournalYear(year=int(y.year), articles=int(y.articles)) for y in g["years"].itertuples()]
            if g["years"] is not None else [],
            topics=[C.TopicShare(topic=code[int(t.topic_id)], count=int(t.count), share=_f(t.share) or 0.0)
                    for t in topics.itertuples()] if topics is not None else [],
            drift=[C.DriftPoint(year=int(d.year), articles=int(d.articles), distance=_f(d.distance))
                   for d in g["drift"].itertuples()] if g["drift"] is not None else [],
            gained=[C.TopicShift(topic=code[int(s.topic_id)], share_before=_f(s.share_before) or 0.0,
                                 share_after=_f(s.share_after) or 0.0)
                    for s in shifts.itertuples() if s.kind == "gained" and int(s.topic_id) in code]
            if shifts is not None else [],
            lost=[C.TopicShift(topic=code[int(s.topic_id)], share_before=_f(s.share_before) or 0.0,
                               share_after=_f(s.share_after) or 0.0)
                  for s in shifts.itertuples() if s.kind == "lost" and int(s.topic_id) in code]
            if shifts is not None else [],
            peers=[C.Peer(id=jid[p.peer_key], similarity=_f(p.similarity) or 0.0) for p in g["peers"].itertuples()]
            if g["peers"] is not None else [],
            completeness=[C.CompletenessItem(key=c.component, rate=_f(c.rate), n=int(c.n))
                          for c in g["comp"].itertuples()] if g["comp"] is not None else [],
            methods=[C.MethodShare(method=m.method, count=int(m.count), share=_f(m.share) or 0.0)
                     for m in g["methods"].sort_values(["count", "method"], ascending=[False, True]).itertuples()]
            if g["methods"] is not None else [],
            opportunities=[C.JournalOpportunity(topic=code[int(o.topic_id)], reason=o.reason,
                                                journal_articles=int(o.journal_articles), hosts=int(o.hosts))
                           for o in g["opps"].itertuples() if int(o.topic_id) in code] if g["opps"] is not None else [])
    si = read_table(work / "13_special_issue.parquet")
    w = meta["windows"]
    files["journals.json"] = C.JournalsFile(
        window=_window(w["W"]), recent=_window(w["R"]), baseline=_window(w["B"]),
        min_articles=int(jcfg["min_articles"]), completeness_weights=dict(jcfg["completeness_weights"]),
        inhouse_flag_share=jcfg["inhouse_flag_share"], special_issue_statuses=list(jcfg["special_issue_statuses"]),
        special_issue_max_hosts=int(jcfg["special_issue_max_hosts"]),
        special_issue_min_host_articles=int(jcfg["special_issue_min_host_articles"]),
        drift_reference_years=int(jcfg["drift_reference_years"]), journals=rows,
        special_issue=[C.SpecialIssueTopic(topic=code[int(s.topic_id)], hosts=int(s.hosts),
                                           top_host_share=_f(s.top_host_share) or 0.0, articles_recent=int(s.articles_R))
                       for s in si.itertuples() if int(s.topic_id) in code])
    return files, jid, names


def methods_file(cfg: dict, work: Path, code: dict[int, str]) -> C.MethodsFile | None:
    topics = _opt(work / "12_methods_topics.parquet")
    if topics is None or not (work / "12_methods_meta.json").exists():
        return None
    mcfg = cfg["methods"]
    meta = json.loads((work / "12_methods_meta.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    notes = _opt(work / "12_methods_notes.parquet")
    note = ({int(n.topic_id): C.Narrative(th=str(n.th), en=str(n.en), source=str(n.source)) for n in notes.itertuples()}
            if notes is not None else {})

    def shares(counts: dict, n_with: int) -> list[C.MethodShare]:
        items = sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0]))
        return [C.MethodShare(method=k, count=int(n), share=round(n / n_with, 4) if n_with else 0.0) for k, n in items]

    by = meta["by_year"]
    return C.MethodsFile(
        lexicon_version=meta["lexicon_version"], window=_window(meta["window"]), articles=int(meta["articles"]),
        with_methods=int(meta["with_methods"]), monoculture_methods=list(mcfg["monoculture_methods"]),
        monoculture_min_share=float(mcfg["monoculture_min_share"]), monoculture_share=_f(meta.get("monoculture_share")),
        min_articles=int(mcfg["min_articles"]), methods=[C.MethodInfo(key=m.key, th=m.th, en=m.en) for m in METHODS],
        corpus=shares(meta["counts"], int(meta["with_methods"])),
        by_year=C.MethodYears(years=by["years"], articles=by["articles"], shares=by["shares"]),
        topics=[C.TopicMethods(
            topic=code[int(t.topic_id)], articles=int(t.articles), with_methods=int(t.with_methods),
            coverage=_f(t.coverage) or 0.0, methods=shares(json.loads(t.counts), int(t.with_methods)),
            sample_size_n=int(t.ss_n), sample_size_median=_f(t.ss_median, 1), sample_size_q1=_f(t.ss_q1, 1),
            sample_size_q3=_f(t.ss_q3, 1), monoculture_share=_f(t.monoculture_share), monoculture=bool(t.monoculture),
            note=note.get(int(t.topic_id)))
            for t in topics.itertuples() if int(t.topic_id) in code])


def match_file(work: Path, code: dict[int, str], jid: dict[str, str]) -> C.MatchIndex | None:
    p = work / "14_match.json"
    if not p.exists():
        return None
    ix = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    return C.MatchIndex(
        analyzer=ix["analyzer"], fold=ix["fold"], docs=ix["docs"], max_terms=ix["max_terms"],
        min_similarity=ix["min_similarity"], fit_topics=ix["fit_topics"], min_query_chars=ix["min_query_chars"],
        grams=ix["grams"], idf=ix["idf"],
        topics=[C.MatchTopic(id=code[int(t["topic_id"])], terms=t["terms"], weights=t["weights"])
                for t in ix["topics"] if int(t["topic_id"]) in code],
        journals=[C.MatchJournal(id=jid[j["journal_key"]], topics=[code[int(t)] for t in j["topics"] if int(t) in code],
                                 shares=[s for t, s in zip(j["topics"], j["shares"]) if int(t) in code])
                  for j in ix["journals"] if j["journal_key"] in jid])
```

```python
def release_snapshot(release: str, files: dict[str, C._Model]) -> dict:
    """What the next release's diff compares with (atlas.diff)."""
    j = files.get("journals.json")
    return make_snapshot(release, [t.model_dump(mode="json") for t in files["topics.json"].topics],
                         files["overview.json"].kpis.model_dump(mode="json"),
                         [x.id for x in j.journals] if j is not None else None)


def release_diff(cfg: dict, release: str, prev: str, files: dict[str, C._Model]) -> C.DiffFile | None:
    snap, source = load_snapshot(cfg, prev)
    if snap is None:
        print(f"[export] previous release {prev}: no snapshot in state_dir and no folder in output/data — "
              "diff.json skipped")
        return None
    lineage = {t.id: {"event": t.lineage.event, "parents": t.lineage.parents} for t in files["topics.json"].topics}
    d = quarter_diff(release_snapshot(release, files), snap, lineage, int(cfg["changes"]["movers"]),
                     float(cfg["changes"]["min_share_delta"]))
    return C.DiffFile(previous_source=source, **d)


```

```python
def _rewrite(rel_dir, path, obj):
    """Replace one published file and keep the manifest's size/sha256 in step (a coherent tampering)."""
    data = json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")
    (rel_dir / path).write_bytes(data)
    man = json.loads((rel_dir / "manifest.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    for f in man["files"]:
        if f["path"] == path:
            f.update(bytes=len(data), sha256=hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest())
    (rel_dir / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(man), encoding="utf-8")


def test_validate_checks_the_editor_modules(two_releases, tmp_path):
    from atlas.validate import validate_release

    data, cfg = _copy(two_releases, tmp_path)
    rel = data / "2026Q2"
    assert validate_release(rel, None, cfg["validation"]).exit_code == 0
    detail = json.loads((rel / "journals/so02.BECJournal.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    _rewrite(rel, "journals/so02.BECJournal.json", {**detail, "peers": [{"id": "so09.Ghost", "similarity": 0.5}]})
    diff = json.loads((rel / "diff.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    _rewrite(rel, "diff.json", {**diff, "movers": [{"topic": "T999999", "share_previous": 0.1, "share_current": 0.2}]})
    rep = validate_release(rel, None, {**cfg["validation"], "max_file_bytes": 100})
    assert rep.exit_code == 2
    assert any("peers not in journals.json: ['so09.Ghost']" in e for e in rep.errors)
    assert any("diff.json: topics not in topics.json: ['T999999']" in e for e in rep.errors)
    assert any("validation.max_file_bytes" in w for w in rep.warnings)


def test_offline_edition_leaves_out_the_match_index(two_releases, tmp_path):

    site = tmp_path / "site"

    (site / "offline-template.html").write_text(TEMPLATE, encoding="utf-8")
    publish({**cfg, "pipeline": {**cfg["pipeline"], "release": "2026Q2"}}, site=site)
    html = (site / "atlas-offline.html").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    files = json.loads(re.search(r'id="atlas-data">(.*?)</script>', html, re.S).group(1))["files"]
    assert "match.json" not in files and "journals.json" in files and "diff.json" in files
    assert (site / "data" / "2026Q2" / "match.json").exists()          # the web edition keeps it
```

```python
def _unknown(ids, known: set[str]) -> list[str]:
    return sorted({i for i in ids if i not in known})


def _check_editors(models: dict, rep: Report) -> None:
    """Cross-file references of the P2 modules (journals, methods, diff, match)."""
    topics = models.get("topics.json")
    if topics is None:
        return
    known = {t.id for t in topics.topics}
    jf = models.get("journals.json")
    jids: set[str] | None = None
    if jf is not None:
        ids = [j.id for j in jf.journals]
        jids = set(ids)
        if len(jids) != len(ids):
            rep.errors.append("journals.json: duplicate journal ids")
        missing = [i for i in ids if f"journals/{i}.json" not in models]
        if missing:
            rep.errors.append(f"{len(missing)} journals have no valid journals/<id>.json (e.g. {missing[0]})")
        if bad := _unknown((s.topic for s in jf.special_issue), known):
            rep.errors.append(f"journals.json: special-issue topics not in topics.json: {bad[:5]}")
    for path, m in models.items():
        if path.startswith("journals/"):
            refs = [t.topic for t in m.topics] + [s.topic for s in m.gained + m.lost] + [o.topic for o in m.opportunities]
            if bad := _unknown(refs, known):
                rep.errors.append(f"{path}: topics not in topics.json: {bad[:5]}")
            if jids is not None and (bad := _unknown((p.id for p in m.peers), jids)):
                rep.errors.append(f"{path}: peers not in journals.json: {bad[:5]}")
        elif path.startswith("topics/") and jids is not None:
            if bad := _unknown((j.id for j in m.journals if j.id), jids):
                rep.errors.append(f"{path}: journal links not in journals.json: {bad[:5]}")
    if (mf := models.get("methods.json")) is not None and (bad := _unknown((t.topic for t in mf.topics), known)):
        rep.errors.append(f"methods.json: topics not in topics.json: {bad[:5]}")
    if (df := models.get("diff.json")) is not None:
        refs = ([t.topic for t in df.new_topics] + [c.topic for c in df.status_changes] + [m.topic for m in df.movers])
        if bad := _unknown(refs, known):
            rep.errors.append(f"diff.json: topics not in topics.json: {bad[:5]}")
        if still := sorted({r.topic for r in df.retired} & known):
            rep.errors.append(f"diff.json: retired topics still in topics.json: {still[:5]}")
    if (mx := models.get("match.json")) is not None:
        if bad := _unknown((t.id for t in mx.topics), known):
            rep.errors.append(f"match.json: topics not in topics.json: {bad[:5]}")
        refs = [t for j in mx.journals for t in j.topics]
        if bad := _unknown(refs, known):
            rep.errors.append(f"match.json: journal topics not in topics.json: {bad[:5]}")
        if jids is not None and (bad := _unknown((j.id for j in mx.journals), jids)):
            rep.errors.append(f"match.json: journals not in journals.json: {bad[:5]}")
```

Replace the whole `offline_bundle` function head and body up to its `return` with:

```python
def offline_bundle(release_dir: str | Path, latest: dict, exclude=()) -> dict:
    """{latest, files: {relative path: parsed JSON}} — what atlas-offline.html carries; paths equal to (or inside
    a folder named by) an entry of `exclude` are left out."""
    root = Path(release_dir)
    skip = tuple(str(e).strip("/") for e in exclude)
    files = {rel: json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
             for p in sorted(root.rglob("*.json"))
             if not any((rel := p.relative_to(root).as_posix()) == e or rel.startswith(f"{e}/") for e in skip)}
    return {"latest": latest, "files": files}
```

In `publish_site` replace `        html = inject_offline(template.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), offline_bundle(release_dir, latest_json))` with


- [ ] **Step 3: Loader.** In `web/src/data/release.ts` insert directly above the comment `/** topics/<id>.json on demand, one request per topic; a failed request may be retried later. */`:

```ts
/** Optional modules of contract 2.1 and the file each page reads first. */
export const MODULE_FILES = { journals: 'journals.json', methods: 'methods.json', diff: 'diff.json', match: 'match.json' } as const
export type ModuleName = keyof typeof MODULE_FILES

export function hasModule(release: AtlasRelease, module: ModuleName | 'journal_detail'): boolean {
  return (release.manifest.modules ?? []).includes(module)
}

/** Any other file of the release (journals.json, journals/<id>.json, methods.json, diff.json, match.json), fetched on
 *  demand and cached; a failed request may be retried later. null = this release (or this offline file) has no such file. */
export function createFileLoader(source: DataSource, release: string): (path: string) => Promise<unknown> {
  const cache = new Map<string, Promise<unknown>>()
  return (path) => {
    let pending = cache.get(path)
    if (!pending) {
      pending = source.file<unknown>(release, path)
      pending.catch(() => cache.delete(path))
      cache.set(path, pending)
    }
    return pending
  }
}
```

In `web/src/data/atlasContext.ts` replace `  | { status: 'ready'; release: AtlasRelease; detail: (id: string) => Promise<TopicDetail | null> }` with `  | { status: 'ready'; release: AtlasRelease; detail: (id: string) => Promise<TopicDetail | null>; file: (path: string) => Promise<unknown> }` and append:

```ts
export type FileState<T> = { status: 'loading' | 'missing' | 'error'; data: null } | { status: 'ready'; data: T }

/** One optional file of the release (a P2 module file), keyed by path so a new path never shows the previous data.
 *  'missing' = the release (or the offline file) does not carry it; null path = nothing to load. */
export function useReleaseFile<T>(path: string | null): FileState<T> {
  const state = useAtlas()
  const loader = state.status === 'ready' ? state.file : null
  const [entry, setEntry] = useState<{ path: string; result: FileState<T> } | null>(null)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!path || !loader) return
    let alive = true
    loader(path).then(
      (value) => {
        if (alive) setEntry({ path, result: value === null || value === undefined ? { status: 'missing', data: null } : { status: 'ready', data: value as T } })
      },
      () => {
        if (alive) setEntry({ path, result: { status: 'error', data: null } })
      },
    )
    return () => {
      alive = false
    }
  }, [path, loader])
  if (!path) return { status: 'missing', data: null }
  return entry && entry.path === path ? entry.result : { status: 'loading', data: null }
}
```

insert the block below directly above `function manifest(release, previous, modules, entries, extra = {}) {`:

```js
// ---- P2 editors' modules (contract 2.1): journals, journals/<id>, methods, diff, match — synthetic, NOT real data
// Journal j of JOURNALS has the OJS path `example${j}` (the article URLs above use it). The registry names 0, 1, 3 and 4;
// 2 and 5 are shown by their OJS path. 4 has too few articles (low_data); 5 joined in 2023 (a new journal for the diff).
const J_IN_REGISTRY = [true, true, false, true, true, false]
const STATUS_ORDER = ['EMERGING', 'WATCH', 'HOT', 'DECLINING', 'STABLE', 'LOW_DATA']
const jId = (j) => `so02.example${j}`
const jName = (j) => (J_IN_REGISTRY[j] ? JOURNALS[j] : `example${j}`)
const METHOD_INFO = [
  ['survey', 'แบบสอบถาม/การสำรวจ', 'Questionnaire / survey'], ['interview', 'การสัมภาษณ์', 'Interviews'],
  ['focus_group', 'การสนทนากลุ่ม', 'Focus groups'], ['experiment', 'การทดลอง/กึ่งทดลอง', 'Experiment / quasi-experiment'],
  ['qualitative', 'การวิจัยเชิงคุณภาพ', 'Qualitative research'], ['mixed', 'การวิจัยแบบผสานวิธี', 'Mixed methods'],
  ['rnd', 'การวิจัยและพัฒนา', 'Research and development'], ['action', 'การวิจัยเชิงปฏิบัติการ', 'Action research'],
  ['case_study', 'กรณีศึกษา', 'Case study'], ['document', 'การวิเคราะห์เอกสาร/เนื้อหา', 'Document / content analysis'],
  ['review', 'การทบทวนอย่างเป็นระบบ/อภิมาน', 'Systematic review / meta-analysis'], ['descriptive_stats', 'สถิติเชิงพรรณนา', 'Descriptive statistics'],
  ['inferential', 'สถิติเชิงอนุมาน', 'Inferential statistics'], ['sem', 'สมการโครงสร้าง/องค์ประกอบ', 'SEM / factor analysis'],
  ['ml', 'การเรียนรู้ของเครื่อง', 'Machine learning'], ['lab', 'การทดลองในห้องปฏิบัติการ', 'Laboratory study'],
]

// the analyzer of web/src/lib/matcher.ts (= pipeline/atlas/matcher.py): fold, then char_wb trigrams
// (regexes built from code points: a literal invisible character in source code is easy to lose or corrupt)
const cp = (...xs) => String.fromCodePoint(...xs)
const DECOMPOSED_AM = new RegExp(cp(0x0e4d, 0x0e32), 'g')
const INVISIBLE = new RegExp(`[${cp(0x200b)}-${cp(0x200d)}${cp(0xfeff)}${cp(0xad)}]`, 'g')
const LATIN_ACCENTS = new RegExp(`[${cp(0x300)}-${cp(0x36f)}]`, 'g')
const THAI_ABOVE = new RegExp(`[${cp(0x0e47)}-${cp(0x0e4c)}]`, 'g')
const foldMatch = (s) =>
  s.normalize('NFC').replace(DECOMPOSED_AM, cp(0x0e33)).replace(INVISIBLE, '').normalize('NFD')
    .replace(LATIN_ACCENTS, '').replace(THAI_ABOVE, '').toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
const trigrams = (folded) => {
  const out = []
  for (const word of folded ? folded.split(' ') : []) {
    const w = [...` ${word} `]
    if (w.length <= 3) out.push(w.join(''))
    else for (let i = 0; i + 3 <= w.length; i++) out.push(w.slice(i, i + 3).join(''))
  }
  return out
}

function buildEditors(files, previousOverview) {
  const topics = files['topics.json'].topics
  const byId = new Map(topics.map((t) => [t.id, t]))
  const groupOf = (j) => GROUPS[j % GROUPS.length][0]
  const jTopics = (j) => topics.filter((t) => t.group === groupOf(j)).slice(0, 5).concat(j === 0 ? [byId.get('T000201')] : [])
  const summaries = JOURNALS.map((name, j) => {
    const low = j === 4
    const windowN = [240, 180, 150, 310, 12, 24][j]
    return {
      id: jId(j), key: `so02:example${j}`, path: `example${j}`,
      name_th: J_IN_REGISTRY[j] && j !== 3 ? name : '', name_en: j === 3 ? name : '',
      issn: '', eissn: '', tci_tier: [1, 2, null, 1, 2, null][j], tci_field: J_IN_REGISTRY[j] ? GROUPS[j % GROUPS.length][2] : '',
      publisher: j === 0 ? ORGS[0] : j === 3 ? ORGS[3] : '', url: J_IN_REGISTRY[j] ? `https://so02.tci-thaijo.org/index.php/example${j}` : '',
      in_registry: J_IN_REGISTRY[j], articles: [520, 410, 300, 640, 30, 24][j], articles_window: windowN, articles_recent: Math.round(windowN * 0.6),
      first_year: j === 5 ? 2023 : 2015, last_year: 2024, active: true, low_data: low, group: groupOf(j),
      completeness: low ? null : [86.4, 71.2, 58.9, 93.1, null, 64.0][j],
      owner: low ? '' : [ORGS[0], ORGS[1], ORGS[2], ORGS[3], '', ORGS[1]][j],
      owner_source: low ? null : j === 0 || j === 3 ? 'registry' : 'inferred', owner_matched: !low,
      inhouse_share: low ? null : [0.42, 0.81, 0.66, 0.12, null, 0.58][j],
      n_orgs: [64, 21, 33, 95, 3, 9][j], effective_orgs: low ? null : [22.5, 3.1, 8.4, 41.7, null, 4.2][j],
      foreign_share: low ? null : [0.03, 0, 0.01, 0.18, null, 0][j], affiliation_coverage: low ? null : [0.97, 0.92, 0.88, 0.99, null, 0.95][j],
      methods_coverage: low ? null : [0.81, 0.77, 0.69, 0.84, null, 0.72][j],
    }
  })
  const special = [
    { topic: 'T000406', hosts: 2, top_host_share: 0.58, articles_recent: byId.get('T000406').metrics.count_recent },
    { topic: 'T000502', hosts: 1, top_host_share: 0.71, articles_recent: byId.get('T000502').metrics.count_recent },
    { topic: 'T000305', hosts: 1, top_host_share: 0.83, articles_recent: byId.get('T000305').metrics.count_recent },
  ]
  files['journals.json'] = {
    schema_version: SCHEMA, window: { start: 2020, end: 2024 }, recent: { start: 2022, end: 2024 }, baseline: { start: 2017, end: 2021 },
    min_articles: 20, completeness_weights: { abstract_en: 0.2, title_en: 0.1, keywords_en: 0.15, doi: 0.2, references: 0.15, refs_romanized: 0.1, affiliations: 0.1 },
    inhouse_flag_share: 0.6, special_issue_statuses: ['EMERGING', 'WATCH'], special_issue_max_hosts: 5, special_issue_min_host_articles: 3, drift_reference_years: 3,
    journals: summaries, special_issue: special,
  }
  const PEERS = { 0: [[2, 0.82], [1, 0.35]], 1: [[5, 0.64], [0, 0.35]], 2: [[0, 0.82]], 3: [], 4: [], 5: [[1, 0.64]] }
  const OPPS = { 0: [['T000502', 'adjacent', 0, 1]], 1: [['T000305', 'in_scope', 2, 1]], 2: [], 3: [['T000406', 'in_scope', 5, 2], ['T000502', 'adjacent', 0, 1]], 4: [], 5: [] }
  const COMPLETE = ['abstract_en', 'title_en', 'keywords_en', 'doi', 'references', 'refs_romanized', 'affiliations']
  JOURNALS.forEach((_, j) => {
    const s = summaries[j]
    const tl = jTopics(j)
    const weights = tl.map((_, i) => 1 / (i + 1.5))
    const wsum = sum(weights) * 1.25
    files[`journals/${jId(j)}.json`] = {
      schema_version: SCHEMA, id: jId(j),
      years: YEARS.filter((y) => y >= s.first_year).map((y, i) => ({ year: y, articles: Math.round(s.articles / (j === 5 ? 2 : 10) * (0.8 + 0.04 * i)) })),
      topics: tl.map((t, i) => ({ topic: t.id, count: Math.round(s.articles_window * weights[i] / wsum), share: r4(weights[i] / wsum) })),
      drift: s.low_data ? [] : [2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024].filter((y) => y >= s.first_year).map((y, i) => ({
        year: y, articles: Math.round(s.articles_window / 5), distance: j === 5 && i === 0 ? null : i === 0 && j !== 3 ? null : r4(j === 3 && y === 2023 ? 0.41 : 0.08 + 0.02 * i),
      })),
      gained: s.low_data ? [] : [{ topic: tl[0].id, share_before: 0.12, share_after: 0.21 }],
      lost: s.low_data ? [] : [{ topic: tl[tl.length - 1].id, share_before: 0.14, share_after: 0.06 }],
      peers: PEERS[j].map(([p, sim]) => ({ id: jId(p), similarity: sim })),
      completeness: s.low_data ? [] : COMPLETE.map((key, k) => ({ key, rate: key === 'refs_romanized' && j === 3 ? null : r4(0.55 + ((j + k) % 5) * 0.1), n: key === 'refs_romanized' && j === 3 ? 0 : s.articles_window })),
      methods: s.low_data ? [] : [['survey', 0.52], ['descriptive_stats', 0.47], ['interview', 0.21], ['inferential', 0.19]].map(([m, sh]) => ({ method: m, count: Math.round(s.articles_window * s.methods_coverage * sh), share: sh })),
      opportunities: OPPS[j].map(([topic, reason, n, hosts]) => ({ topic, reason, journal_articles: n, hosts })),
    }
  })
  // topic details link their journals by id
  for (const [path, d] of Object.entries(files)) {
    if (!path.startsWith('topics/')) continue
    d.journals = d.journals.map((c) => {
      const j = JOURNALS.indexOf(c.key)
      return j >= 0 ? { key: jName(j), count: c.count, id: jId(j) } : c
    })
  }

  // methods.json — E11 monoculture on two education/social topics
  const share = (k, i) => r4(Math.max(0.01, [0.52, 0.18, 0.06, 0.09, 0.14, 0.05, 0.07, 0.03, 0.08, 0.06, 0.02, 0.47, 0.24, 0.07, 0.04, 0.03][k] + 0.002 * i))
  const methodTopics = topics.filter((t) => t.status !== 'LOW_DATA').slice(0, 14).map((t, i) => {
    const mono = t.id === 'T000203' || t.id === 'T000204'
    const articles = Math.max(30, t.metrics.count_recent)
    const withM = Math.round(articles * 0.78)
    const ms = (mono ? [['survey', 0.82], ['descriptive_stats', 0.76], ['inferential', 0.12]] : [['survey', 0.44], ['interview', 0.31], ['descriptive_stats', 0.29], ['qualitative', 0.22]])
      .map(([m, sh]) => ({ method: m, count: Math.round(withM * sh), share: r4(sh + 0.001 * i) }))
    return {
      topic: t.id, articles, with_methods: withM, coverage: r4(withM / articles), methods: ms,
      sample_size_n: Math.round(withM * 0.6), sample_size_median: [400, 385, 220, 120, 30][i % 5], sample_size_q1: [200, 180, 120, 60, 15][i % 5], sample_size_q3: [420, 400, 380, 240, 45][i % 5],
      monoculture_share: r4(mono ? 0.71 + 0.01 * i : 0.18 + 0.01 * i), monoculture: mono,
      note: i < 2 ? { th: `บทความในหัวข้อ${t.th}ที่ระบุวิธีวิจัยได้ ${Math.round(ms[0].share * 100)}% ใช้แบบสอบถาม (ข้อมูลตัวอย่าง)`, en: `${Math.round(ms[0].share * 100)}% of the articles on ${t.en} that name a method use a questionnaire (sample data).`, source: 'template' } : null,
    }
  })
  files['methods.json'] = {
    schema_version: SCHEMA, lexicon_version: 'fixture1', window: { start: 2020, end: 2024 }, articles: 18234, with_methods: 14002,
    monoculture_methods: ['survey', 'descriptive_stats'], monoculture_min_share: 0.6, monoculture_share: 0.27, min_articles: 30,
    methods: METHOD_INFO.map(([key, th, en]) => ({ key, th, en })),
    corpus: METHOD_INFO.map(([key], k) => ({ method: key, count: Math.round(14002 * share(k, 0)), share: share(k, 0) })).sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count),
    by_year: {
      years: YEARS, articles: YEARS.map((_, i) => 900 + 60 * i),
      shares: Object.fromEntries(METHOD_INFO.map(([key], k) => [key, YEARS.map((_, i) => r4(key === 'survey' ? 0.58 - 0.012 * i : key === 'ml' ? 0.005 + 0.006 * i : share(k, i)))])),
    },
    topics: methodTopics,
  }

  // diff.json — what changed since 2026Q3
  const ov = files['overview.json']
  const KPI = ['articles', 'assigned_articles', 'topics', 'journals', 'institutions']
  const prevStatus = { T000201: 'WATCH', T000406: 'STABLE', T000104: 'HOT', T000305: 'STABLE', T000503: 'STABLE' }
  files['diff.json'] = {
    schema_version: SCHEMA, release: RELEASE, previous_release: PREVIOUS, previous_source: 'state',
    kpis: KPI.map((key) => ({ key, previous: previousOverview.kpis[key], current: ov.kpis[key] })),
    new_topics: [{ topic: 'T000701', event: 'new', parents: [] }, { topic: 'T000106', event: 'split', parents: ['T000105'] }, { topic: 'T000706', event: 'merge', parents: ['T000703', 'T000704'] }],
    retired: [{ topic: 'T000999', th: 'หนังสือพิมพ์รายวัน', en: 'Daily newspapers' }],
    status_changes: Object.entries(prevStatus).map(([topic, previous]) => ({ topic, previous, current: byId.get(topic).status }))
      .sort((a, b) => STATUS_ORDER.indexOf(a.current) - STATUS_ORDER.indexOf(b.current) || a.topic.localeCompare(b.topic)),
    movers: [...topics].filter((t) => t.status !== 'LOW_DATA').sort((a, b) => b.metrics.share_recent - a.metrics.share_recent).slice(0, 6)
      .map((t, i) => ({ topic: t.id, share_previous: r6(t.metrics.share_recent * (i % 2 ? 1.12 : 0.86)), share_current: t.metrics.share_recent })),
    journals_compared: true, new_journals: [jId(5)],
  }

  // match.json — one "document" per topic (names, keywords, description), weighted like the pipeline
  const docs = topics.map((t) => trigrams(foldMatch([t.th, t.en, ...t.keywords, t.description].join(' '))))
  const df = new Map()
  for (const d of docs) for (const g of new Set(d)) df.set(g, (df.get(g) ?? 0) + 1)
  const vecs = docs.map((d) => {
    const tf = new Map()
    for (const g of d) tf.set(g, (tf.get(g) ?? 0) + 1)
    const w = [...tf].map(([g, n]) => [g, (1 + Math.log(n)) * (Math.log((1 + docs.length) / (1 + df.get(g))) + 1)])
    w.sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1] || (a[0] < b[0] ? -1 : 1))
    const top = w.slice(0, 60)
    const norm = Math.sqrt(sum(top.map(([, v]) => v * v)))
    return top.map(([g, v]) => [g, v / norm])
  })
  const grams = [...new Set(vecs.flatMap((v) => v.map(([g]) => g)))].sort()
  const pos = new Map(grams.map((g, i) => [g, i]))
  files['match.json'] = {
    schema_version: SCHEMA, analyzer: 'char_wb3', fold: 'atlas-fold-1', docs: docs.length, max_terms: 60, min_similarity: 0.05, fit_topics: 5, min_query_chars: 40,
    grams, idf: grams.map((g) => r4(Math.log((1 + docs.length) / (1 + df.get(g))) + 1)),
    topics: topics.map((t, i) => ({ id: t.id, terms: vecs[i].map(([g]) => pos.get(g)), weights: vecs[i].map(([, v]) => r4(v)) })),
    journals: JOURNALS.map((_, j) => j).filter((j) => !summaries[j].low_data)
      .map((j) => ({ id: jId(j), topics: files[`journals/${jId(j)}.json`].topics.map((x) => x.topic), shares: files[`journals/${jId(j)}.json`].topics.map((x) => x.share) })),
  }
  return ['journals', 'journal_detail', 'methods', 'diff', 'match']
}
```

```ts
  'mod.error': { th: 'โหลดข้อมูลส่วนนี้ไม่สำเร็จ ลองโหลดหน้าใหม่', en: 'This part could not be loaded. Try reloading the page.' },
```

In `web/src/strings/index.ts` insert `import { modules } from './modules'` below `import { emerging } from './emerging'`, replace `brief } as const` with `brief, modules } as const` and `...brief }` with `...brief, ...modules }`. Create `web/src/components/ModuleFile.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useReleaseFile } from '../data/atlasContext'
import { hasModule, type AtlasRelease, type ModuleName } from '../data/release'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { EmptyState } from './EmptyState'
import { PageHeader } from './PageHeader'

/**
 * A page built on one optional module file (contract 2.1). Renders `children(data)` when the file is loaded;
 * otherwise the page h1 and why there is nothing to show (module absent, left out of the offline file, failed).
 * Like RequireRelease: put hooks in a component rendered by the children function, never in the function itself.
 */
export function ModuleFile<T>({ release, module, path, title, children }: {
  release: AtlasRelease
  module: ModuleName
  path: string
  title: string
  children: (data: T) => ReactNode
}) {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  const present = hasModule(release, module)
  const file = useReleaseFile<T>(present ? path : null)
  if (present && file.status === 'ready') return <>{children(file.data)}</>
  return (
    <div className="container">
      <PageHeader title={title} />
      {!present ? (
        <EmptyState title={t('mod.absent')}><p>{t('mod.absentBody')}</p></EmptyState>
      ) : file.status === 'loading' ? (
        <p className="loading" role="status">{t('loading')}</p>
      ) : file.status === 'missing' ? (
        release.source === 'embedded' ? (
          <EmptyState title={t('mod.offline')}><p>{t('mod.offlineBody')}</p></EmptyState>
        ) : (
          <EmptyState title={t('mod.missingFile')} />
        )
      ) : (
        <p className="note">{t('mod.error')}</p>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Module downloads on /data.** Create `web/src/pages/data/ModuleDownloads.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Section } from '../../components/Section'
import { useAtlas } from '../../data/atlasContext'
import { hasModule, MODULE_FILES, type AtlasRelease, type ModuleName } from '../../data/release'
import { dataUrl } from '../../data/source'
import { downloadText } from '../../lib/csv'
import { fmtNum } from '../../lib/format'
import { useI18n } from '../../lib/i18n'
import type { StrKey } from '../../strings'

const INFO: Record<ModuleName, StrKey> = {
  journals: 'data.file.journals',
  methods: 'data.file.methods',
  diff: 'data.file.diff',
  match: 'data.file.match',
}

/** Files of the optional modules: direct links on the web; on the offline file, rebuilt from the embedded data. */
export function ModuleDownloads({ release }: { release: AtlasRelease }) {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  const atlas = useAtlas()
  const load = atlas.status === 'ready' ? atlas.file : null
  const [absent, setAbsent] = useState<ReadonlySet<string>>(new Set())
  const rel = release.overview.release
  const sizes = new Map((release.manifest.files ?? []).map((f) => [f.path, f.bytes]))
  const present = (Object.keys(MODULE_FILES) as ModuleName[]).filter((m) => hasModule(release, m))
  if (present.length === 0) return null
  return (
    <Section id="modules" title={t('data.mod.h')} intro={t('data.mod.intro')}>
      <ul className="dl-list">
        {present.map((m) => {
          const path = MODULE_FILES[m]
          const name = `atlas-${rel}-${path}`
          return (
            <li key={m}>
              <div>
                <code>{path}</code>
                <span className="dl-note">{t(INFO[m])}{sizes.has(path) ? ` · ${fmtNum((sizes.get(path) ?? 0) / 1024, 0)} KB` : ''}</span>
              </div>
              {absent.has(path) ? (
                <span className="dl-note">{t('mod.offline')}</span>
              ) : release.source === 'http' ? (
                <a className="btn-secondary" href={dataUrl(import.meta.env.BASE_URL, `${rel}/${path}`)} download={name} aria-label={`${t('data.dl.json')}: ${name}`}>{t('data.dl.json')}</a>
              ) : (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="btn-secondary"
                  aria-label={`${t('data.dl.json')}: ${name}`}
                  onClick={() => {
                    void load?.(path).then((v) => {
                      if (v) downloadText(name, JSON.stringify(v), 'application/json')
                      else setAbsent((prev) => new Set(prev).add(path))     // e.g. match.json, left out of the offline file
                    })
                  }}
                >
                  {t('data.dl.json')}
                </button>
              )}
            </li>
          )
        })}
      </ul>
    </Section>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Helpers.** Create `web/src/lib/journals.ts`:

```ts
import type { JournalSummary } from '../data/types'
import type { Lang } from './i18n'
import { foldText } from './search'

export interface ShownName {
  text: string
  /** language of `text` when it differs from the UI language, for a lang attribute; null = same / not a name */
  lang: Lang | null
  /** true when no registry name exists and the OJS path is shown instead */
  isPath: boolean
}

type Named = Pick<JournalSummary, 'name_th' | 'name_en' | 'path'>

/** The registry name in the UI language, else the other language, else the OJS path (never an empty title). */
export function journalName(j: Named, lang: Lang): ShownName {
  const own = lang === 'en' ? j.name_en : j.name_th
  const other = lang === 'en' ? j.name_th : j.name_en
  if (own) return { text: own, lang: null, isPath: false }
  if (other) return { text: other, lang: lang === 'en' ? 'th' : 'en', isPath: false }
  return { text: j.path, lang: null, isPath: true }
}

/** The name in the other language when both exist and differ (shown under the title). */
export function otherJournalName(j: Named, lang: Lang): string {
  const own = lang === 'en' ? j.name_en : j.name_th
  const other = lang === 'en' ? j.name_th : j.name_en
  return own && other && own !== other ? other : ''
}

/** In-house share above journals.inhouse_flag_share (published in journals.json; null = no mark). */
export function inhouseFlag(j: Pick<JournalSummary, 'inhouse_share'>, threshold: number | null | undefined): boolean {
  return threshold !== null && threshold !== undefined && j.inhouse_share !== null && j.inhouse_share !== undefined && j.inhouse_share > threshold
}

export type TierFilter = 'all' | '1' | '2' | '3' | 'none'

/** Journals whose names, OJS path, key or ISSN contain the query (Thai folded, tone marks ignored) and match the tier. */
export function filterJournals(journals: readonly JournalSummary[], query: string, tier: TierFilter): JournalSummary[] {
  const q = foldText(query)
  return journals.filter((j) => {
    if (tier === 'none' ? j.tci_tier != null : tier !== 'all' && j.tci_tier !== Number(tier)) return false
    if (!q) return true
    return [j.name_th, j.name_en, j.path, j.key, j.issn, j.eissn].some((v) => v && foldText(v).includes(q))
  })
}
```

```ts
export const JOURNAL_SORTS = ['name', 'tier', 'articles', 'completeness', 'inhouse', 'orgs', 'foreign'] as const
export type JournalSort = (typeof JOURNAL_SORTS)[number]
export const TIERS = ['all', '1', '2', '3', 'none'] as const
export interface JournalsUrlState {
  q: string
  tier: (typeof TIERS)[number]
  sort: JournalSort
  dir: 'asc' | 'desc'
}
export const journalsCodec = makeCodec<JournalsUrlState>({
  q: text('q', 80),
  tier: oneOf('tier', TIERS, 'all'),
  sort: oneOf('sort', JOURNAL_SORTS, 'articles'),
  dir: oneOf('dir', ['asc', 'desc'] as const, 'desc'),
})
export const journalCodec = makeCodec<{ drift: View }>({ drift: oneOf('drift', VIEWS, 'chart') })
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Components and styles.** Create `web/src/components/ShareBar.tsx`:

```tsx
import { fmtPct } from '../lib/format'

/** A share 0–1 as a thin bar plus its number (the number carries the meaning; the bar is decoration). */
export function ShareBar({ value, digits = 0, max = 1 }: { value: number | null | undefined; digits?: number; max?: number }) {
  if (value === null || value === undefined || !Number.isFinite(value)) return <span className="sbar-na">–</span>
  const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (value / (max || 1)) * 100))
  return (
    <span className="sbar-cell">
      <span className="sbar" aria-hidden="true">
        <span className="sbar-fill" style={{ width: `${pct}%` }} />
      </span>
      <span className="sbar-num">{fmtPct(value, digits)}</span>
    </span>
  )
}
```

Create `web/src/components/JournalLink.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router'
import type { JournalSummary } from '../data/types'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { journalName } from '../lib/journals'

/** A journal's name linking to /journals/:id, with its OJS name (or "no full name yet") underneath. */
export function JournalLink({ journal }: { journal: Pick<JournalSummary, 'id' | 'name_th' | 'name_en' | 'path'> }) {
  const { lang, t } = useI18n()
  const n = journalName(journal, lang)
  return (
    <>
      <Link to={`/journals/${journal.id}`} className="topic-link">
        <span lang={n.lang ?? undefined}>{n.text}</span>
      </Link>
      <span className="j-path">{n.isPath ? t('jr.noName') : t('jr.ojs', { path: journal.path })}</span>
    </>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'

/** Shown while a lazily loaded page's code arrives (Suspense fallback). */
export function RouteLoading() {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  return (
    <div className="container">
      <p className="loading" role="status">{t('loading')}</p>
    </div>

}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The page.** Create `web/src/pages/JournalsPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { DataTable, type Column } from '../components/DataTable'
import { JournalLink } from '../components/JournalLink'
import { ModuleFile } from '../components/ModuleFile'
import { PageHeader } from '../components/PageHeader'
import { RequireRelease } from '../components/RequireRelease'
import { Section } from '../components/Section'
import { ShareBar } from '../components/ShareBar'
import { TopicLink } from '../components/TopicLink'
import { groupName, MODULE_FILES, type AtlasRelease } from '../data/release'
import type { JournalSummary, JournalsFile, SpecialIssueTopic, Topic } from '../data/types'
import { DASH, fmtInt, fmtNum, fmtPct } from '../lib/format'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { filterJournals, inhouseFlag, journalName } from '../lib/journals'
import { statusName } from '../lib/topicText'
import { JOURNAL_SORTS, journalsCodec, TIERS, type JournalSort } from '../lib/urlState'
import { useUrlState } from '../lib/useUrlState'

export default function JournalsPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  return (
    <RequireRelease title={t('jr.title')}>
      {(release) => (
        <ModuleFile<JournalsFile> release={release} module="journals" path={MODULE_FILES.journals} title={t('jr.title')}>
          {(file) => <Journals release={release} file={file} />}
        </ModuleFile>
      )}
    </RequireRelease>
  )
}

const isSort = (v: string): v is JournalSort => (JOURNAL_SORTS as readonly string[]).includes(v)

function Journals({ release, file }: { release: AtlasRelease; file: JournalsFile }) {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n()
  const [state, update] = useUrlState(journalsCodec)
  const w = file.window
  const rows = filterJournals(file.journals, state.q, state.tier)
  const flagAt = file.inhouse_flag_share ?? null
  const columns: Column<JournalSummary>[] = [
    { key: 'id', header: 'id', csvHeader: 'journal_id', cell: () => null, csv: (j) => j.id, csvOnly: true },
    {
      key: 'name', header: t('col.journal'), rowHeader: true, cell: (j) => <JournalLink journal={j} />, sortValue: (j) => journalName(j, lang).text,
      csvCols: [{ header: 'name_th', value: (j) => j.name_th ?? '' }, { header: 'name_en', value: (j) => j.name_en ?? '' }, { header: 'ojs_path', value: (j) => j.path }],
    },
    { key: 'tier', csvHeader: 'tci_tier', header: t('col.tier'), numeric: true, firstDir: 'asc', cell: (j) => (j.tci_tier ? fmtInt(j.tci_tier) : DASH), sortValue: (j) => j.tci_tier ?? 9, csv: (j) => j.tci_tier ?? null },
    { key: 'articles', csvHeader: 'articles_window', header: t('col.articlesW'), numeric: true, cell: (j) => fmtInt(j.articles_window), sortValue: (j) => j.articles_window },
    {
      key: 'completeness', csvHeader: 'completeness', header: t('col.completeness'), numeric: true,
      cell: (j) => (j.completeness == null ? (j.low_data ? t('jr.lowData') : DASH) : fmtNum(j.completeness, 1)), sortValue: (j) => j.completeness ?? null,
    },
    {
      key: 'inhouse', header: t('col.inhouse'), numeric: true, sortValue: (j) => j.inhouse_share ?? null,
      cell: (j) => (
        <>
          <ShareBar value={j.inhouse_share} />
          {j.owner_source === 'inferred' && j.inhouse_share != null ? <span className="flag">{t('jr.inferred')}</span> : null}
          {inhouseFlag(j, flagAt) ? <span className="flag">{t('jr.flag')}</span> : null}
        </>
      ),
      csvCols: [{ header: 'inhouse_share', value: (j) => j.inhouse_share ?? null }, { header: 'owner_source', value: (j) => j.owner_source ?? '' }],
    },
    {
      key: 'orgs', header: t('col.effOrgs'), numeric: true, cell: (j) => fmtNum(j.effective_orgs ?? null, 1), sortValue: (j) => j.effective_orgs ?? null,
      csvCols: [{ header: 'effective_orgs', value: (j) => j.effective_orgs ?? null }, { header: 'n_orgs', value: (j) => j.n_orgs ?? 0 }],
    },
    { key: 'foreign', csvHeader: 'foreign_share', header: t('col.foreign'), numeric: true, cell: (j) => fmtPct(j.foreign_share ?? null, 0), sortValue: (j) => j.foreign_share ?? null },
    { key: 'group', csvHeader: 'group', header: t('col.field'), cell: (j) => (j.group ? groupName(release, j.group, lang) : DASH), csv: (j) => j.group ?? '' },
  ]
  const valid = columns.some((c) => c.key === state.sort && c.sortValue)
  return (
    <div className="container">
      <PageHeader title={t('jr.title')} lede={t('jr.lede', { a: w.start, b: w.end })} />
      <div className="j-tools">
        <label>
          {t('jr.search')}
          <input type="search" value={state.q} onChange={(e) => update({ q: e.target.value })} />
        </label>
        <label>
          {t('jr.tier')}
          <select value={state.tier} onChange={(e) => update({ tier: (TIERS as readonly string[]).includes(e.target.value) ? (e.target.value as (typeof TIERS)[number]) : 'all' })}>
            {TIERS.map((tier) => (
              <option key={tier} value={tier}>{tier === 'all' ? t('jr.tier.all') : tier === 'none' ? t('jr.tier.none') : t('jr.tier.n', { n: tier })}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
        <p className="hint" role="status">{t('jr.count', { n: fmtInt(rows.length), total: fmtInt(file.journals.length) })}</p>
      </div>
      <Section id="journals" title={t('jr.table', { a: w.start, b: w.end })}>
        <DataTable
          caption={t('table.n', { n: 1, title: t('jr.table', { a: w.start, b: w.end }) })}
          rows={rows}
          columns={columns}
          rowKey={(j) => j.id}
          sort={valid ? { key: state.sort, dir: state.dir } : { key: 'articles', dir: 'desc' }}
          onSort={(s) => update({ sort: isSort(s.key) ? s.key : 'articles', dir: s.dir })}
          csvFilename={`atlas-${release.overview.release}-journals.csv`}
          empty={t('jr.none')}
          footnote={flagAt != null ? t('jr.foot', { p: fmtPct(flagAt, 0), n: fmtInt(file.min_articles) }) : t('jr.footNoFlag', { n: fmtInt(file.min_articles) })}
        />
      </Section>
      <SpecialIssues release={release} file={file} />
    </div>
  )
}

function SpecialIssues({ release, file }: { release: AtlasRelease; file: JournalsFile }) {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n()
  const rows = (file.special_issue ?? []).filter((s) => release.topicById.has(s.topic))
  if (rows.length === 0) return null
  const topicOf = (s: SpecialIssueTopic) => release.topicById.get(s.topic) as Topic
  const columns: Column<SpecialIssueTopic>[] = [
    { key: 'topic', csvHeader: 'topic_id', header: t('col.topic'), rowHeader: true, cell: (s) => <TopicLink topic={topicOf(s)} showStatus />, sortValue: (s) => s.topic, csv: (s) => s.topic },
    { key: 'hosts', csvHeader: 'hosts', header: t('col.hosts'), numeric: true, firstDir: 'asc', cell: (s) => fmtInt(s.hosts), sortValue: (s) => s.hosts },
    { key: 'top', csvHeader: 'top_host_share', header: t('col.topHost'), numeric: true, cell: (s) => fmtPct(s.top_host_share, 0), sortValue: (s) => s.top_host_share },
    { key: 'articles', csvHeader: 'articles_recent', header: t('col.articlesR'), numeric: true, cell: (s) => fmtInt(s.articles_recent), sortValue: (s) => s.articles_recent },
  ]
  const r = file.recent
  return (
    <Section
      id="special-issues"
      title={t('jr.si.h')}
      intro={t('jr.si.intro', {
        statuses: (file.special_issue_statuses ?? []).map((s) => statusName(t, s)).join(lang === 'en' ? ' or ' : 'หรือ'),
        max: fmtInt(file.special_issue_max_hosts ?? 0), min: fmtInt(file.special_issue_min_host_articles ?? 0), a: r.start, b: r.end,
      })}
    >
      <DataTable
        caption={t('table.n', { n: 2, title: t('jr.si.table') })}
        rows={rows}
        columns={columns}
        rowKey={(s) => s.topic}
        defaultSort={{ key: 'hosts', dir: 'asc' }}
        csvFilename={`atlas-${release.overview.release}-special-issues.csv`}
      />
    </Section>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Lazy route + navigation.** Replace `web/src/App.tsx` with:

```tsx
import { lazy, Suspense, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { Navigate, Route, Routes } from 'react-router'
import { Layout } from './components/Layout'
import { RouteLoading } from './components/RouteLoading'
import AboutPage from './pages/AboutPage'
import AtlasPage from './pages/AtlasPage'
import BriefPage from './pages/BriefPage'
import ComparePage from './pages/ComparePage'
import DataPage from './pages/DataPage'
import EmergingPage from './pages/EmergingPage'
import NotFoundPage from './pages/NotFoundPage'
import OverviewPage from './pages/OverviewPage'
import TopicPage from './pages/TopicPage'

// The editors' pages load on demand: their code (and the data they fetch) stays out of the first download.
const JournalsPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/JournalsPage'))

function Lazy({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return <Suspense fallback={<RouteLoading />}>{children}</Suspense>
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/brief" element={<BriefPage />} />
      <Route element={<Layout />}>
        <Route index element={<Navigate to="/overview" replace />} />
        <Route path="overview" element={<OverviewPage />} />
        <Route path="atlas" element={<AtlasPage />} />
        <Route path="topics/:id" element={<TopicPage />} />
        <Route path="emerging" element={<EmergingPage />} />
        <Route path="journals" element={<Lazy><JournalsPage /></Lazy>} />
        <Route path="compare" element={<ComparePage />} />
        <Route path="data" element={<DataPage />} />
        <Route path="about" element={<AboutPage />} />
        <Route path="*" element={<NotFoundPage />} />
      </Route>
    </Routes>
  )
}
```

In `web/src/components/Header.tsx` below `  { to: '/emerging', key: 'nav.emerging' },` insert `  { to: '/journals', key: 'nav.journals' },`.

- [ ] **Step 7: Bundle budget.** Create `web/scripts/check-bundle.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Bundle budget for the Pages build (run after `npm run build`):
//   * the entry chunk named in dist/index.html stays under the budget, gzip (default 180 KB, or --budget <kb>)
//   * every page App.tsx loads with lazy(() => import('./pages/X')) is its own chunk (not in the first download)
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs'
import { join } from 'node:path'
import { gzipSync } from 'node:zlib'

const LAZY = [...readFileSync(join('src', 'App.tsx'), 'utf8').matchAll(/lazy\(\(\) => import\('\.\/pages\/(\w+)'\)\)/g)].map((m) => m[1])
const arg = process.argv.indexOf('--budget')
const budget = Number(arg > 0 ? process.argv[arg + 1] : 180)
const html = readFileSync(join('dist', 'index.html'), 'utf8')
const entry = /src="\.\/assets\/([^"]+\.js)"/.exec(html)?.[1]
if (!entry) throw new Error('no entry script in dist/index.html — run npm run build first')
const assets = readdirSync(join('dist', 'assets'))
const kb = (f) => gzipSync(readFileSync(join('dist', 'assets', f))).length / 1024
const size = kb(entry)
const missing = LAZY.filter((name) => !assets.some((f) => f.startsWith(`${name}-`) && f.endsWith('.js')))
console.log(`[bundle] entry ${entry}: ${size.toFixed(1)} KB gzip (budget ${budget} KB)`)
for (const f of assets.filter((a) => LAZY.some((n) => a.startsWith(`${n}-`)))) console.log(`[bundle] lazy ${f}: ${kb(f).toFixed(1)} KB gzip`)
if (missing.length) {
  console.error(`[bundle] not split into lazy chunks: ${missing.join(', ')}`)
  process.exit(1)
}
if (size > budget) {
  console.error(`[bundle] entry chunk is over budget by ${(size - budget).toFixed(1)} KB`)
  process.exit(1)
}
```

In `web/package.json` "scripts" add `"check:bundle": "node scripts/check-bundle.mjs",` below `"build": …`.

- [ ] **Step 2: The page.** Create `web/src/pages/JournalPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router'
import { MultiLineChart } from '../charts/MultiLineChart'
import { DataTable, type Column } from '../components/DataTable'
import { Figure } from '../components/Figure'
import { JournalLink } from '../components/JournalLink'
import { ModuleFile } from '../components/ModuleFile'
import { OnThisPage } from '../components/OnThisPage'
import { PageHeader } from '../components/PageHeader'
import { RequireRelease } from '../components/RequireRelease'
import { Section } from '../components/Section'
import { ShareBar } from '../components/ShareBar'
import { TopicLink } from '../components/TopicLink'
import { useReleaseFile } from '../data/atlasContext'
import { hasModule, MODULE_FILES, type AtlasRelease } from '../data/release'
import type { CompletenessItem, DriftPoint, JournalDetail, JournalSummary, JournalsFile, MethodShare, MethodsFile, Topic, TopicShare } from '../data/types'
import { DASH, fmtInt, fmtNum, fmtPct } from '../lib/format'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { inhouseFlag, journalName, otherJournalName } from '../lib/journals'
import { journalCodec } from '../lib/urlState'
import { useUrlState } from '../lib/useUrlState'
import type { StrKey } from '../strings'

export default function JournalPage() {
  const { id } = useParams()
  const { t } = useI18n()
  return (
    <RequireRelease title={t('jr.title')}>
      {(release) => (
        <ModuleFile<JournalsFile> release={release} module="journals" path={MODULE_FILES.journals} title={t('jr.title')}>
          {(file) => <JournalView release={release} file={file} id={id ?? ''} />}
        </ModuleFile>
      )}
    </RequireRelease>
  )
}

function JournalView({ release, file, id }: { release: AtlasRelease; file: JournalsFile; id: string }) {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  const journal = file.journals.find((j) => j.id === id)
  if (!journal) {
    return (
      <div className="container">
        <PageHeader title={t('jr.notFound', { id: id || DASH })} lede={t('jr.notFound.body')} />
        <p className="mt-6"><Link to="/journals">{t('jr.back')}</Link></p>
      </div>
    )
  }
  return <JournalContent key={journal.id} release={release} file={file} journal={journal} />
}

function JournalContent({ release, file, journal }: { release: AtlasRelease; file: JournalsFile; journal: JournalSummary }) {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n()
  const detailFile = useReleaseFile<JournalDetail>(`journals/${journal.id}.json`)
  const detail = detailFile.status === 'ready' ? detailFile.data : null
  const [state, update] = useUrlState(journalCodec)
  const methodsFile = useReleaseFile<MethodsFile>(hasModule(release, 'methods') ? MODULE_FILES.methods : null)
  const methodInfo = new Map((methodsFile.status === 'ready' ? methodsFile.data.methods : []).map((m) => [m.key, m]))
  const w = file.window
  const shown = journalName(journal, lang)
  const other = otherJournalName(journal, lang)
  const source = t('source.line', { release: release.overview.release, v: release.definitionVersion })
  const topicOf = (tid: string): Topic | undefined => release.topicById.get(tid)
  const byId = new Map(file.journals.map((j) => [j.id, j]))
  const topics = (detail?.topics ?? []).filter((s) => topicOf(s.topic))
  const drift = detail?.drift ?? []
  const comp = detail?.completeness ?? []
  const methods = detail?.methods ?? []
  const peers = (detail?.peers ?? []).filter((p) => byId.has(p.id))
  const opps = (detail?.opportunities ?? []).filter((o) => topicOf(o.topic))
  const gained = (detail?.gained ?? []).filter((s) => topicOf(s.topic))
  const lost = (detail?.lost ?? []).filter((s) => topicOf(s.topic))
  const owner = journal.owner || ''
  const toc = [
    { id: 'numbers', label: t('jr.numbers.h') },
    ...(topics.length ? [{ id: 'topics', label: t('jr.topics.h') }] : []),
    ...(drift.some((d) => d.distance != null) ? [{ id: 'drift', label: t('jr.drift.h') }] : []),
    ...(comp.length ? [{ id: 'completeness', label: t('jr.comp.h') }] : []),
    ...(peers.length ? [{ id: 'peers', label: t('jr.peers.h') }] : []),
    ...(methods.length ? [{ id: 'methods', label: t('jr.methods.h') }] : []),
    ...(opps.length ? [{ id: 'opportunities', label: t('jr.opp.h') }] : []),
  ]
  const share = (s: TopicShare) => s.share
  const topicColumns: Column<TopicShare>[] = [
    { key: 'topic', csvHeader: 'topic_id', header: t('col.topic'), rowHeader: true, cell: (s) => <TopicLink topic={topicOf(s.topic) as Topic} showStatus />, sortValue: (s) => s.topic, csv: (s) => s.topic },
    { key: 'count', csvHeader: 'articles', header: t('col.articles'), numeric: true, cell: (s) => fmtInt(s.count), sortValue: (s) => s.count },
    { key: 'share', csvHeader: 'share', header: t('col.share'), numeric: true, cell: (s) => <ShareBar value={s.share} max={Math.max(...topics.map(share), 0.0001)} />, sortValue: share },
  ]
  const driftColumns: Column<DriftPoint>[] = [
    { key: 'year', csvHeader: 'year', header: t('col.year'), rowHeader: true, cell: (d) => String(d.year), sortValue: (d) => d.year },
    { key: 'articles', csvHeader: 'articles', header: t('col.articles'), numeric: true, cell: (d) => fmtInt(d.articles), sortValue: (d) => d.articles },
    { key: 'distance', csvHeader: 'distance', header: t('col.distance'), numeric: true, cell: (d) => fmtNum(d.distance ?? null, 2), sortValue: (d) => d.distance ?? null },
  ]
  const compColumns: Column<CompletenessItem>[] = [
    { key: 'key', csvHeader: 'element', header: t('col.element'), rowHeader: true, cell: (c) => t(`comp.${c.key}` as StrKey), csv: (c) => c.key },
    { key: 'rate', csvHeader: 'rate', header: t('col.rate'), numeric: true, cell: (c) => <ShareBar value={c.rate} />, sortValue: (c) => c.rate ?? null },
    { key: 'n', csvHeader: 'articles', header: t('col.n'), numeric: true, cell: (c) => fmtInt(c.n), sortValue: (c) => c.n },
  ]
  type PeerRow = { journal: JournalSummary; similarity: number | null }
  const peerRows: PeerRow[] = [{ journal, similarity: null }, ...peers.map((p) => ({ journal: byId.get(p.id) as JournalSummary, similarity: p.similarity }))]
  const peerColumns: Column<PeerRow>[] = [
    { key: 'name', csvHeader: 'journal_id', header: t('col.journal'), rowHeader: true, cell: (r) => (r.similarity === null ? <strong>{t('jr.this')}</strong> : <JournalLink journal={r.journal} />), csv: (r) => r.journal.id },
    { key: 'sim', csvHeader: 'similarity', header: t('col.similarity'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => (r.similarity === null ? DASH : fmtNum(r.similarity, 2)), csv: (r) => r.similarity },
    { key: 'articles', csvHeader: 'articles_window', header: t('col.articlesW'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => fmtInt(r.journal.articles_window), csv: (r) => r.journal.articles_window },
    { key: 'completeness', csvHeader: 'completeness', header: t('col.completeness'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => fmtNum(r.journal.completeness ?? null, 1), csv: (r) => r.journal.completeness ?? null },
    { key: 'inhouse', csvHeader: 'inhouse_share', header: t('col.inhouse'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => fmtPct(r.journal.inhouse_share ?? null, 0), csv: (r) => r.journal.inhouse_share ?? null },
    { key: 'orgs', csvHeader: 'effective_orgs', header: t('col.effOrgs'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => fmtNum(r.journal.effective_orgs ?? null, 1), csv: (r) => r.journal.effective_orgs ?? null },
    { key: 'foreign', csvHeader: 'foreign_share', header: t('col.foreign'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => fmtPct(r.journal.foreign_share ?? null, 0), csv: (r) => r.journal.foreign_share ?? null },
  ]
  const methodName = (key: string) => {
    const m = methodInfo.get(key)
    return m ? (lang === 'en' ? m.en : m.th) : key
  }
  const methodColumns: Column<MethodShare>[] = [
    { key: 'method', csvHeader: 'method', header: t('col.method'), rowHeader: true, cell: (m) => methodName(m.method), csv: (m) => m.method },
    { key: 'count', csvHeader: 'articles', header: t('col.articles'), numeric: true, cell: (m) => fmtInt(m.count), sortValue: (m) => m.count },
    { key: 'share', csvHeader: 'share', header: t('col.share'), numeric: true, cell: (m) => <ShareBar value={m.share} />, sortValue: (m) => m.share },
  ]
  const rel = release.overview.release
  return (
    <div className="container">
      <PageHeader title={shown.text} titleLang={shown.lang ?? undefined}>
        {other ? <p className="tp-alt" lang={lang === 'en' ? 'th' : 'en'}>{other}</p> : null}
        <div className="j-tags">
          {journal.tci_tier ? <span className="tp-badge">{t('jr.tier.n', { n: journal.tci_tier })}</span> : null}
          <span className="tp-sub">{shown.isPath ? t('jr.noName') : t('jr.ojs', { path: journal.path })}</span>
          {journal.issn ? <span>ISSN {journal.issn}</span> : null}
          {journal.eissn ? <span>e-ISSN {journal.eissn}</span> : null}
          {journal.publisher ? <span>{t('jr.publisher', { p: journal.publisher })}</span> : null}
          {journal.first_year && journal.last_year ? <span>{t('jr.years', { a: journal.first_year, b: journal.last_year })}</span> : null}
          {journal.url ? <a href={journal.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">{t('jr.site')}<span className="sr-only"> {t('newTab')}</span></a> : null}
        </div>
      </PageHeader>
      <div className="layout">
        <div className="main-col">
          <Section id="numbers" title={t('jr.numbers.h')}>
            {journal.low_data ? <p className="note">{t('jr.lowDataBody', { a: w.start, b: w.end, n: fmtInt(file.min_articles) })}</p> : null}
            <dl className="summary">
              <div>
                <dt>{t('col.articlesW')}</dt>
                <dd>{fmtInt(journal.articles_window)}<small>{t('jr.articlesSub', { a: w.start, b: w.end, all: fmtInt(journal.articles) })}</small></dd>
              </div>
              <div>
                <dt>{t('col.completeness')}</dt>
                <dd>{fmtNum(journal.completeness ?? null, 1)}<small>{t('jr.completenessSub')}</small></dd>
              </div>
              <div>
                <dt>{t('col.inhouse')}</dt>
                <dd>
                  {fmtPct(journal.inhouse_share ?? null, 0)}
                  {inhouseFlag(journal, file.inhouse_flag_share) ? <span className="flag">{t('jr.flag')}</span> : null}
                  <small>{owner ? t(journal.owner_source === 'inferred' ? 'jr.inhouseSubInferred' : 'jr.inhouseSub', { o: owner }) : t('jr.noOwner')}</small>
                </dd>
              </div>
              <div>
                <dt>{t('col.effOrgs')}</dt>
                <dd>{fmtNum(journal.effective_orgs ?? null, 1)}<small>{t('jr.orgsSub', { n: fmtInt(journal.n_orgs ?? 0), e: fmtNum(journal.effective_orgs ?? null, 1) })}</small></dd>
              </div>
              <div>
                <dt>{t('col.foreign')}</dt>
                <dd>{fmtPct(journal.foreign_share ?? null, 0)}<small>{t('jr.foreignSub')} · {t('jr.coverageSub', { p: fmtPct(journal.affiliation_coverage ?? null, 0) })}</small></dd>
              </div>
            </dl>
          </Section>

          {topics.length ? (
            <Section id="topics" title={t('jr.topics.h')} intro={t('jr.topics.intro', { a: w.start, b: w.end })}>
              <DataTable caption={t('table.n', { n: 1, title: t('jr.topics.table') })} rows={topics} columns={topicColumns} rowKey={(s) => s.topic} defaultSort={{ key: 'count', dir: 'desc' }} csvFilename={`atlas-${rel}-${journal.id}-topics.csv`} />
              {gained.length ? <Shifts title={t('jr.gained', { r: `${file.recent.start}–${file.recent.end}`, b: `${file.baseline.start}–${file.baseline.end}` })} rows={gained} release={release} /> : null}
              {lost.length ? <Shifts title={t('jr.lost', { r: `${file.recent.start}–${file.recent.end}`, b: `${file.baseline.start}–${file.baseline.end}` })} rows={lost} release={release} /> : null}
            </Section>
          ) : null}

          {drift.some((d) => d.distance != null) ? (
            <Section id="drift" title={t('jr.drift.h')} intro={t('jr.drift.intro', { n: fmtInt(file.drift_reference_years ?? 0) })}>
              <Figure
                id="fig-drift"
                number={1}
                title={t('jr.drift.fig')}
                source={source}
                view={state.drift}
                onView={(v) => update({ drift: v })}
                chart={<MultiLineChart years={drift.map((d) => d.year)} series={[{ id: 'drift', label: t('jr.drift.series'), values: drift.map((d) => d.distance ?? null) }]} format={(v) => fmtNum(v, 2)} axisFormat={(v) => fmtNum(v, 1)} label={t('jr.drift.fig')} />}
                table={<DataTable caption={t('table.n', { n: 2, title: t('jr.drift.table') })} rows={drift} columns={driftColumns} rowKey={(d) => String(d.year)} stack={false} csvFilename={`atlas-${rel}-${journal.id}-drift.csv`} />}
              />
            </Section>
          ) : null}

          {comp.length ? (
            <Section id="completeness" title={t('jr.comp.h')} intro={t('jr.comp.intro')}>
              <DataTable caption={t('table.n', { n: 3, title: t('jr.comp.table') })} rows={comp} columns={compColumns} rowKey={(c) => c.key} stack={false} csvFilename={`atlas-${rel}-${journal.id}-completeness.csv`} />
            </Section>
          ) : null}

          {peers.length ? (
            <Section id="peers" title={t('jr.peers.h')} intro={t('jr.peers.intro')}>
              <DataTable caption={t('table.n', { n: 4, title: t('jr.peers.table') })} rows={peerRows} columns={peerColumns} rowKey={(r) => r.journal.id} csvFilename={`atlas-${rel}-${journal.id}-peers.csv`} />
            </Section>
          ) : null}

          {methods.length ? (
            <Section id="methods" title={t('jr.methods.h')} intro={t('jr.methods.intro', { p: fmtPct(journal.methods_coverage ?? null, 0) })}>
              <DataTable caption={t('table.n', { n: 5, title: t('jr.methods.table') })} rows={methods} columns={methodColumns} rowKey={(m) => m.method} stack={false} csvFilename={`atlas-${rel}-${journal.id}-methods.csv`} />
            </Section>
          ) : null}

          {opps.length ? (
            <Section id="opportunities" title={t('jr.opp.h')} intro={t('jr.opp.intro')}>
              <ul className="opp-list">
                {opps.map((o) => (
                  <li key={o.topic}>
                    <TopicLink topic={topicOf(o.topic) as Topic} showStatus />
                    <span className="topic-sub">{t(o.reason === 'in_scope' ? 'jr.opp.in_scope' : 'jr.opp.adjacent', { n: fmtInt(o.journal_articles), h: fmtInt(o.hosts) })}</span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </Section>
          ) : null}
          {detailFile.status === 'loading' ? <p className="loading" role="status">{t('loading')}</p> : null}
        </div>
        <aside className="rail" aria-label={t('onThisPage')}>
          <OnThisPage items={toc} />
          <p className="mt-6"><Link to="/journals">{t('jr.back')}</Link></p>
        </aside>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function Shifts({ title, rows, release }: { title: string; rows: readonly { topic: string; share_before: number; share_after: number }[]; release: AtlasRelease }) {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  return (
    <>
      <h3 className="mt-6">{title}</h3>
      <ul className="shift-list">
        {rows.map((s) => (
          <li key={s.topic}>
            <TopicLink topic={release.topicById.get(s.topic) as Topic} />{' '}
            <span className="topic-sub">{t('jr.shift', { from: fmtPct(s.share_before, 0), to: fmtPct(s.share_after, 0) })}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </>
  )
}
```
