```ts
import { defineStrings } from './define'

/** /methods (E11, D7). */
export const methods = defineStrings({
  'nav.methods': { th: 'วิธีวิจัย', en: 'Methods' },
  'me.title': { th: 'วิธีวิจัย', en: 'Research methods' },
  'me.lede': { th: 'วิธีวิจัยที่บทความระบุไว้ในชื่อเรื่องและบทคัดย่อ อ่านด้วยคลังคำภาษาไทยและอังกฤษ ช่วง {a}–{b} ใช้ดูภาพรวมและความหลากหลายของวิธี ไม่ใช่การตัดสินคุณภาพของบทความ', en: 'The methods articles name in their titles and abstracts, read with a Thai and English lexicon, {a}–{b}. Use it to see the mix and diversity of methods, not to judge any article’s quality.' },
  'me.kpi.coverage': { th: 'บทความที่ระบุวิธีได้', en: 'Articles naming a method' },
  'me.kpi.coverageSub': { th: '{n} จาก {all} บทความ', en: '{n} of {all} articles' },
  'me.kpi.mono': { th: 'ใช้เฉพาะ {methods}', en: 'Only {methods}' },
  'me.kpi.monoSub': { th: 'ของบทความที่ระบุวิธีได้', en: 'of the articles naming a method' },
  'me.trend.h': { th: 'วิธีวิจัยรายปี', en: 'Methods by year' },
  'me.trend.fig': { th: 'สัดส่วนบทความที่ใช้วิธีที่พบบ่อยที่สุด 4 วิธี {a}–{b}', en: 'Share of articles using the four most common methods, {a}–{b}' },
  'me.trend.table': { th: 'สัดส่วนบทความที่ใช้แต่ละวิธีรายปี', en: 'Share of articles using each method, by year' },
  'me.corpus.h': { th: 'วิธีวิจัยทั้งคลัง', en: 'Methods across the corpus' },
  'me.corpus.table': { th: 'วิธีวิจัยของบทความ {a}–{b}', en: 'Methods of the articles, {a}–{b}' },
  'me.topics.h': { th: 'วิธีวิจัยรายหัวข้อ', en: 'Methods by topic' },
  'me.topics.intro': { th: 'หัวข้อที่มีบทความอย่างน้อย {n} เรื่องในช่วง {a}–{b} · “วิธีกระจุก” = บทความที่ระบุวิธีได้อย่างน้อย {p} ใช้เฉพาะ {methods}', en: 'Topics with at least {n} articles in {a}–{b}. “Narrow methods” = at least {p} of the articles naming a method use only {methods}.' },
  'me.topics.table': { th: 'วิธีวิจัยของแต่ละหัวข้อ', en: 'Methods of each topic' },
  'me.onlyMono': { th: 'แสดงเฉพาะหัวข้อที่วิธีกระจุก', en: 'Only topics with narrow methods' },
  'me.mono': { th: 'วิธีกระจุก', en: 'Narrow methods' },
  'col.coverage': { th: 'ระบุวิธีได้', en: 'Method named' },
  'col.topMethods': { th: 'วิธีที่พบบ่อย', en: 'Most used methods' },
  'col.sample': { th: 'ขนาดตัวอย่าง (มัธยฐาน)', en: 'Sample size (median)' },
  'col.mono': { th: 'ใช้เฉพาะวิธีหลัก', en: 'Only the common pair' },
  'me.sampleRange': { th: '{m} (ช่วงกลาง {q1}–{q3})', en: '{m} (middle half {q1}–{q3})' },
  'me.notes.h': { th: 'บันทึกวิธีวิจัยของหัวข้อ', en: 'Notes on methods by topic' },
  'me.lexicon.h': { th: 'คลังคำวิธีวิจัย', en: 'The methods lexicon' },
  'me.lexicon.intro': { th: 'วิธีที่ระบบอ่านได้ (ฉบับ {v}) บทความหนึ่งอาจใช้หลายวิธี สัดส่วนจึงรวมกันเกิน 100% ได้ ขนาดตัวอย่างอ่านจากข้อความเช่น “กลุ่มตัวอย่าง 400 คน” หรือ “n = 400”', en: 'The methods the system can read (version {v}). An article may use several, so shares can add up to more than 100%. Sample sizes are read from text such as “a sample of 400” or “n = 400”.' },
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The page.** Create `web/src/pages/MethodsPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { MultiLineChart, type LineSeries } from '../charts/MultiLineChart'
import { DataTable, type Column } from '../components/DataTable'
import { Figure } from '../components/Figure'
import { ModuleFile } from '../components/ModuleFile'
import { PageHeader } from '../components/PageHeader'
import { RequireRelease } from '../components/RequireRelease'
import { Section } from '../components/Section'
import { ShareBar } from '../components/ShareBar'
import { TopicLink } from '../components/TopicLink'
import { MODULE_FILES, type AtlasRelease } from '../data/release'
import type { MethodShare, MethodsFile, Topic, TopicMethods } from '../data/types'
import { DASH, fmtInt, fmtPct } from '../lib/format'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { methodsCodec } from '../lib/urlState'
import { useUrlState } from '../lib/useUrlState'

export default function MethodsPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  return (
    <RequireRelease title={t('me.title')}>
      {(release) => (
        <ModuleFile<MethodsFile> release={release} module="methods" path={MODULE_FILES.methods} title={t('me.title')}>
          {(file) => <Methods release={release} file={file} />}
        </ModuleFile>
      )}
    </RequireRelease>
  )
}

function Methods({ release, file }: { release: AtlasRelease; file: MethodsFile }) {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n()
  const [state, update] = useUrlState(methodsCodec)
  const info = new Map(file.methods.map((m) => [m.key, m]))
  const mName = (key: string) => {
    const m = info.get(key)
    return m ? (lang === 'en' ? m.en : m.th) : key
  }
  const w = file.window
  const monoNames = file.monoculture_methods.map(mName).join(' + ')
  const rel = release.overview.release
  const source = t('source.line', { release: rel, v: release.definitionVersion })
  const by = file.by_year
  const top4 = [...file.corpus].sort((a, b) => b.count - a.count).slice(0, 4).filter((m) => by.shares[m.method])
  const series: LineSeries[] = top4.map((m, i) => ({ id: m.method, label: mName(m.method), values: by.shares[m.method], slot: i }))
  const yearRows = by.years.map((year, i) => ({ year, i }))
  const yearColumns: Column<{ year: number; i: number }>[] = [
    { key: 'year', csvHeader: 'year', header: t('col.year'), rowHeader: true, cell: (r) => String(r.year), csv: (r) => r.year },
    { key: 'n', csvHeader: 'articles_with_method', header: t('col.coverage'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => fmtInt(by.articles[r.i]), csv: (r) => by.articles[r.i] },
    ...Object.keys(by.shares).map((k): Column<{ year: number; i: number }> => ({ key: k, csvHeader: k, header: mName(k), numeric: true, cell: (r) => fmtPct(by.shares[k][r.i], 0), csv: (r) => by.shares[k][r.i] })),
  ]
  const corpusColumns: Column<MethodShare>[] = [
    { key: 'method', csvHeader: 'method', header: t('col.method'), rowHeader: true, cell: (m) => mName(m.method), sortValue: (m) => mName(m.method), csv: (m) => m.method },
    { key: 'count', csvHeader: 'articles', header: t('col.articles'), numeric: true, cell: (m) => fmtInt(m.count), sortValue: (m) => m.count },
    { key: 'share', csvHeader: 'share', header: t('col.share'), numeric: true, cell: (m) => <ShareBar value={m.share} />, sortValue: (m) => m.share },
  ]
  const topicRows = file.topics.filter((r) => release.topicById.has(r.topic) && (state.mono === 'all' || r.monoculture))
  const topicOf = (r: TopicMethods) => release.topicById.get(r.topic) as Topic
  const topicColumns: Column<TopicMethods>[] = [
    { key: 'topic', csvHeader: 'topic_id', header: t('col.topic'), rowHeader: true, cell: (r) => <TopicLink topic={topicOf(r)} showStatus />, sortValue: (r) => r.topic, csv: (r) => r.topic },
    { key: 'articles', csvHeader: 'articles', header: t('col.articles'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => fmtInt(r.articles), sortValue: (r) => r.articles },
    { key: 'coverage', csvHeader: 'coverage', header: t('col.coverage'), numeric: true, cell: (r) => fmtPct(r.coverage, 0), sortValue: (r) => r.coverage },
    {
      key: 'top', header: t('col.topMethods'), cell: (r) => r.methods.slice(0, 2).map((m) => `${mName(m.method)} ${fmtPct(m.share, 0)}`).join(' · ') || DASH,
      csv: (r) => r.methods.map((m) => `${m.method}:${m.share}`).join(';'), csvHeader: 'methods',
    },
    {
      key: 'sample', csvHeader: 'sample_size_median', header: t('col.sample'), numeric: true, sortValue: (r) => r.sample_size_median ?? null,
      cell: (r) => (r.sample_size_median == null ? DASH : t('me.sampleRange', { m: fmtInt(r.sample_size_median), q1: fmtInt(r.sample_size_q1 ?? null), q3: fmtInt(r.sample_size_q3 ?? null) })),
    },
    {
      key: 'mono', csvHeader: 'monoculture_share', header: t('col.mono'), numeric: true, sortValue: (r) => r.monoculture_share ?? null,
      cell: (r) => (
        <>
          <ShareBar value={r.monoculture_share} />
          {r.monoculture ? <span className="flag">{t('me.mono')}</span> : null}
        </>
      ),
    },
  ]
  const notes = file.topics.filter((r) => r.note && release.topicById.has(r.topic))
  return (
    <div className="container">
      <PageHeader title={t('me.title')} lede={t('me.lede', { a: w.start, b: w.end })} />
      <dl className="summary">
        <div>
          <dt>{t('me.kpi.coverage')}</dt>
          <dd>{fmtPct(file.articles ? file.with_methods / file.articles : null, 0)}<small>{t('me.kpi.coverageSub', { n: fmtInt(file.with_methods), all: fmtInt(file.articles) })}</small></dd>
        </div>
        <div>
          <dt>{t('me.kpi.mono', { methods: monoNames })}</dt>
          <dd>{fmtPct(file.monoculture_share ?? null, 0)}<small>{t('me.kpi.monoSub')}</small></dd>
        </div>
      </dl>
      {series.length && by.years.length ? (
        <Section id="trend" title={t('me.trend.h')}>
          <Figure
            id="fig-methods"
            number={1}
            title={t('me.trend.fig', { a: by.years[0], b: by.years[by.years.length - 1] })}
            source={source}
            view={state.trend}
            onView={(v) => update({ trend: v })}
            chart={<MultiLineChart years={by.years} series={series} format={(v) => fmtPct(v, 0)} axisFormat={(v) => fmtPct(v, 0)} label={t('me.trend.fig', { a: by.years[0], b: by.years[by.years.length - 1] })} />}
            table={<DataTable caption={t('table.n', { n: 1, title: t('me.trend.table') })} rows={yearRows} columns={yearColumns} rowKey={(r) => String(r.year)} stack={false} wide csvFilename={`atlas-${rel}-methods-by-year.csv`} />}
          />
        </Section>
      ) : null}
      <Section id="corpus" title={t('me.corpus.h')}>
        <DataTable caption={t('table.n', { n: 2, title: t('me.corpus.table', { a: w.start, b: w.end }) })} rows={file.corpus} columns={corpusColumns} rowKey={(m) => m.method} defaultSort={{ key: 'count', dir: 'desc' }} csvFilename={`atlas-${rel}-methods.csv`} />
      </Section>
      {file.topics.length ? (
        <Section
          id="topics"
          title={t('me.topics.h')}
          intro={t('me.topics.intro', { n: fmtInt(file.min_articles), a: w.start, b: w.end, p: fmtPct(file.monoculture_min_share, 0), methods: monoNames })}
        >
          <label className="check">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={state.mono === 'only'} onChange={(e) => update({ mono: e.target.checked ? 'only' : 'all' })} />
            {t('me.onlyMono')}
          </label>
          <DataTable caption={t('table.n', { n: 3, title: t('me.topics.table') })} rows={topicRows} columns={topicColumns} rowKey={(r) => r.topic} defaultSort={{ key: 'mono', dir: 'desc' }} csvFilename={`atlas-${rel}-methods-by-topic.csv`} />
        </Section>
      ) : null}
      {notes.length ? (
        <Section id="notes" title={t('me.notes.h')}>
          <ul className="opp-list">
            {notes.map((r) => (
              <li key={r.topic}>
                <TopicLink topic={topicOf(r)} />
                <span>{lang === 'en' ? r.note?.en : r.note?.th}</span>
                <span className="headline-src">{t(r.note?.source === 'llm' ? 'autoSummary.llm' : 'autoSummary.template')}</span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Section>
      ) : null}
      <Section id="lexicon" title={t('me.lexicon.h')} intro={t('me.lexicon.intro', { v: file.lexicon_version })}>
        <ul className="kw-list">
          {file.methods.map((m) => <li key={m.key}>{lang === 'en' ? m.en : m.th}</li>)}
        </ul>
      </Section>
    </div>
  )
}
```

import { defineStrings } from './define'

/** /changes (E5) and its link on /overview. */
export const changes = defineStrings({
  'nav.changes': { th: 'อะไรเปลี่ยน', en: 'What changed' },
  'ch.title': { th: 'อะไรเปลี่ยนไปตั้งแต่รอบก่อน', en: 'What changed since the last release' },
  'ch.lede': { th: 'รอบ {release} เทียบกับรอบ {prev}: หัวข้อใหม่ หัวข้อที่ยุติ หัวข้อที่เปลี่ยนสถานะ และหัวข้อที่สัดส่วนเปลี่ยนมากที่สุด', en: 'Release {release} compared with {prev}: new and retired topics, status changes and the biggest movers.' },

- [ ] **Step 3: The page.** Create `web/src/pages/ChangesPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router'
import { DataTable, type Column } from '../components/DataTable'
import { EmptyState } from '../components/EmptyState'
import { JournalLink } from '../components/JournalLink'
import { ModuleFile } from '../components/ModuleFile'
import { PageHeader } from '../components/PageHeader'
import { RequireRelease } from '../components/RequireRelease'
import { Section } from '../components/Section'
import { StatusChip } from '../components/StatusGlyph'
import { TopicLink } from '../components/TopicLink'
import { useReleaseFile } from '../data/atlasContext'
import { hasModule, MODULE_FILES, type AtlasRelease } from '../data/release'
import type { DiffFile, JournalsFile, KpiChange, Mover, StatusChange, Topic } from '../data/types'
import { fmtInt, fmtPct, fmtPp, kpiDelta } from '../lib/format'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { STATUSES } from '../lib/status'
import { statusName } from '../lib/topicText'
import type { StrKey } from '../strings'

export default function ChangesPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  return (
    <RequireRelease title={t('ch.title')}>
      {(release) =>
        !hasModule(release, 'diff') && !release.overview.previous_release ? (
          <div className="container">
            <PageHeader title={t('ch.title')} />
            <EmptyState title={t('ch.noPrevious')}><p>{t('ch.noPreviousBody')}</p></EmptyState>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <ModuleFile<DiffFile> release={release} module="diff" path={MODULE_FILES.diff} title={t('ch.title')}>
            {(diff) => <Changes release={release} diff={diff} />}
          </ModuleFile>
        )
      }
    </RequireRelease>
  )
}

function Changes({ release, diff }: { release: AtlasRelease; diff: DiffFile }) {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n()
  const rel = release.overview.release
  const topicOf = (id: string) => release.topicById.get(id)
  const known = (id: string): boolean => release.topicById.has(id)
  const order = (s: string) => STATUSES.indexOf(s as (typeof STATUSES)[number])
  const kpiColumns: Column<KpiChange>[] = [
    { key: 'key', csvHeader: 'kpi', header: t('col.kpi'), rowHeader: true, cell: (k) => t(`kpi.${k.key}` as StrKey), csv: (k) => k.key },
    { key: 'previous', csvHeader: 'previous', header: t('col.previous'), numeric: true, cell: (k) => fmtInt(k.previous), csv: (k) => k.previous },
    { key: 'current', csvHeader: 'current', header: t('col.current'), numeric: true, cell: (k) => fmtInt(k.current), csv: (k) => k.current },
    { key: 'change', header: t('col.change'), numeric: true, cell: (k) => kpiDelta(k.current, k.previous, 'count', lang) ?? '' },
  ]
  const statusRows = diff.status_changes.filter((c) => known(c.topic))
  const statusColumns: Column<StatusChange>[] = [
    { key: 'topic', csvHeader: 'topic_id', header: t('col.topic'), rowHeader: true, cell: (c) => <TopicLink topic={topicOf(c.topic) as Topic} />, sortValue: (c) => c.topic, csv: (c) => c.topic },
    {
      key: 'change', header: t('col.status'), sortValue: (c) => order(c.current) * 10 + order(c.previous),
      cell: (c) => (
        <span className="st-change">
          <StatusChip status={c.previous} label={statusName(t, c.previous)} small />
          <span aria-hidden="true">→</span>
          <span className="sr-only">{t('to')}</span>
          <StatusChip status={c.current} label={statusName(t, c.current)} small />
        </span>
      ),
      csvCols: [{ header: 'previous', value: (c) => c.previous }, { header: 'current', value: (c) => c.current }],
    },
  ]
  const movers = diff.movers.filter((m) => known(m.topic))
  const moverColumns: Column<Mover>[] = [
    { key: 'topic', csvHeader: 'topic_id', header: t('col.topic'), rowHeader: true, cell: (m) => <TopicLink topic={topicOf(m.topic) as Topic} showStatus />, sortValue: (m) => m.topic, csv: (m) => m.topic },
    { key: 'previous', csvHeader: 'share_previous', header: t('col.previous'), numeric: true, cell: (m) => fmtPct(m.share_previous, 2), sortValue: (m) => m.share_previous },
    { key: 'current', csvHeader: 'share_current', header: t('col.current'), numeric: true, cell: (m) => fmtPct(m.share_current, 2), sortValue: (m) => m.share_current },
    { key: 'change', header: t('col.change'), numeric: true, cell: (m) => fmtPp((m.share_current - m.share_previous) * 100, lang), sortValue: (m) => Math.abs(m.share_current - m.share_previous) },
  ]
  const fresh = diff.new_topics.filter((n) => known(n.topic))
  const journalsLinked = hasModule(release, 'journals')
  const jf = useReleaseFile<JournalsFile>(journalsLinked ? MODULE_FILES.journals : null)
  const journalById = new Map((jf.status === 'ready' ? jf.data.journals : []).map((j) => [j.id, j]))
  return (
    <div className="container">
      <PageHeader title={t('ch.title')} lede={t('ch.lede', { release: diff.release, prev: diff.previous_release })}>
        {diff.previous_source === 'output' ? <p className="note">{t('ch.fromOutput', { prev: diff.previous_release })}</p> : null}
      </PageHeader>
      {diff.kpis.length ? (
        <Section id="kpis" title={t('ch.kpi.h')}>
          <DataTable caption={t('table.n', { n: 1, title: t('ch.kpi.table') })} rows={diff.kpis} columns={kpiColumns} rowKey={(k) => k.key} stack={false} csvFilename={`atlas-${rel}-changes-totals.csv`} />
        </Section>
      ) : null}
      <Section id="status" title={t('ch.status.h')}>
        <DataTable caption={t('table.n', { n: 2, title: t('ch.status.table') })} rows={statusRows} columns={statusColumns} rowKey={(c) => c.topic} defaultSort={{ key: 'change', dir: 'asc' }} empty={t('ch.status.none')} csvFilename={`atlas-${rel}-status-changes.csv`} />
      </Section>
      {fresh.length ? (
        <Section id="new" title={t('ch.new.h')}>
          <ul className="new-list">
            {fresh.map((n) => (
              <li key={n.topic}>
                <TopicLink topic={topicOf(n.topic) as Topic} showStatus />
                <span className="topic-sub">
                  {t(n.event === 'split' ? 'ch.new.split' : n.event === 'merge' ? 'ch.new.merge' : 'ch.new.new')}
                  {(n.parents ?? []).map((p, i) => {
                    const parent = topicOf(p)
                    return <span key={p}>{i ? ', ' : ' '}{parent ? <TopicLink topic={parent} /> : p}</span>
                  })}
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Section>
      ) : null}
      {diff.retired.length ? (
        <Section id="retired" title={t('ch.retired.h')} intro={t('ch.retired.intro')}>
          <ul className="new-list">
            {diff.retired.map((r) => (
              <li key={r.topic}>
                <span lang={lang === 'en' && !r.en ? 'th' : undefined}>{(lang === 'en' ? r.en || r.th : r.th || r.en) || r.topic}</span>
                <span className="topic-sub">{r.topic}</span>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </Section>
      ) : null}
      {movers.length ? (
        <Section id="movers" title={t('ch.movers.h')} intro={t('ch.movers.intro')}>
          <DataTable caption={t('table.n', { n: 3, title: t('ch.movers.table') })} rows={movers} columns={moverColumns} rowKey={(m) => m.topic} defaultSort={{ key: 'change', dir: 'desc' }} csvFilename={`atlas-${rel}-movers.csv`} />
        </Section>
      ) : null}
      {diff.journals_compared && (diff.new_journals ?? []).length ? (
        <Section id="journals" title={t('ch.journals.h')}>
          <ul className="kw-list">
            {(diff.new_journals ?? []).map((id) => {
              const j = journalById.get(id)
              return <li key={id}>{j ? <JournalLink journal={j} /> : journalsLinked ? <Link to={`/journals/${id}`}>{id}</Link> : id}</li>
            })}
          </ul>
        </Section>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  )
}
```

import { bundleOf } from '../../test/fixtureData'
import type { MatchIndex, TopicsFile } from '../data/types'

const index = files['match.json'] as MatchIndex
const topics = (files['topics.json'] as TopicsFile).topics

describe('matcher', () => {
  it('folds and cuts trigrams exactly like the pipeline', () => {
    const cases = JSON.parse(readFileSync(PARITY, 'utf8')) as { text: string; fold: string; grams: string[] }[]
    expect(cases.length).toBeGreaterThan(5)
    for (const c of cases) {
      expect(foldText(c.text), c.text).toBe(c.fold)
      expect(analyze(c.text), c.text).toEqual(c.grams)
    }
    expect(charGrams('ab')).toEqual([' ab', 'ab '])
    expect(queryLength('  ผู้สูงอายุ  ')).toBe(Array.from('ผูสูงอายุ').length)
  })

  it('ranks the topic whose words were pasted first, and journals that publish it', () => {
    const p = prepareIndex(index)
    for (const id of ['T000201', 'T000406', 'T000502']) {
      const t = topics.find((x) => x.id === id)!
      const hits = rankTopics(p, `${t.th} ${(t.keywords ?? []).join(' ')}`)
      expect(hits[0].id, id).toBe(id)
      expect(hits.every((h, i) => i === 0 || hits[i - 1].score >= h.score)).toBe(true)
    }
    const hits = rankTopics(p, 'AI เพื่อการศึกษา ปัญญาประดิษฐ์ generative AI teaching')

    expect(journals.every((j, i) => i === 0 || journals[i - 1].fit >= j.fit)).toBe(true)
  })

  it('returns nothing for text the index does not know', () => {
    expect(rankTopics(prepareIndex(index), 'zzzz qqqq')).toEqual([])
    expect(rankJournals(index, [])).toEqual([])
  })
})

- [ ] **Step 2: The matcher.** Create `web/src/lib/matcher.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * "Match my abstract" (R3) and "where to publish" (R4), entirely in the browser: no model download, nothing sent.
 * The analyzer is the pipeline's (pipeline/atlas/matcher.py): foldText (search.ts) then char_wb trigrams — every
 * space-separated word padded with one space each side, every 3-character slice (a padded word of ≤ 3 characters
 * is one gram). pipeline/tests/fixtures/match_parity.json pins both implementations to the same output.
 * Query weight = (1 + ln tf) · idf over the grams the index knows; topics are ranked by cosine similarity;
 * journals by Σ over the closest fit_topics topics of (similarity share) × (the topic's share of the journal).
 */
import type { MatchIndex } from '../data/types'
import { foldText } from './search'

export function charGrams(folded: string): string[] {
  const out: string[] = []
  for (const word of folded ? folded.split(' ') : []) {
    const w = Array.from(` ${word} `)
    if (w.length <= 3) out.push(w.join(''))
    else for (let i = 0; i + 3 <= w.length; i++) out.push(w.slice(i, i + 3).join(''))
  }
  return out
}

export function analyze(text: string): string[] {
  return charGrams(foldText(text))
}

/** Characters that count toward match.min_query_chars (folded, spaces collapsed). */
export function queryLength(text: string): number {
  return Array.from(foldText(text)).length
}

export interface PreparedIndex {
  index: MatchIndex
  pos: Map<string, number>
}

export function prepareIndex(index: MatchIndex): PreparedIndex {
  return { index, pos: new Map(index.grams.map((g, i) => [g, i])) }
}

/** Unit-length query vector over the index's grams (gram position → weight). */
export function queryVector(p: PreparedIndex, text: string): Map<number, number> {
  const tf = new Map<number, number>()
  for (const g of analyze(text)) {
    const i = p.pos.get(g)
    if (i !== undefined) tf.set(i, (tf.get(i) ?? 0) + 1)
  }
  const q = new Map<number, number>()
  let norm = 0
  for (const [i, n] of tf) {
    const v = (1 + Math.log(n)) * p.index.idf[i]
    q.set(i, v)
    norm += v * v
  }
  norm = Math.sqrt(norm) || 1
  for (const [i, v] of q) q.set(i, v / norm)
  return q
}

export interface TopicHit {
  id: string
  score: number
}

/** Topics with cosine ≥ index.min_similarity, best first (ties by id). */
export function rankTopics(p: PreparedIndex, text: string, limit = 10): TopicHit[] {
  const q = queryVector(p, text)
  if (q.size === 0) return []
  const hits: TopicHit[] = []
  for (const t of p.index.topics) {
    let s = 0
    for (let k = 0; k < t.terms.length; k++) s += (q.get(t.terms[k]) ?? 0) * t.weights[k]
    if (s >= p.index.min_similarity) hits.push({ id: t.id, score: s })
  }
  hits.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score || a.id.localeCompare(b.id))
  return hits.slice(0, limit)
}

export interface JournalFit {
  id: string
  fit: number
  /** the closest topics this journal publishes in, with their share of its articles */
  topics: { id: string; share: number }[]
}

/** Journals ranked by fit = Σ_t w_t · share_j(t) over the fit_topics best topic hits, w_t = score_t / Σ score. */
export function rankJournals(index: MatchIndex, hits: readonly TopicHit[], limit = 8): JournalFit[] {
  const top = hits.slice(0, index.fit_topics)
  const total = top.reduce((a, h) => a + h.score, 0)
  if (!total) return []
  const weight = new Map(top.map((h) => [h.id, h.score / total]))
  const out: JournalFit[] = []
  for (const j of index.journals ?? []) {
    let fit = 0
    const topics: { id: string; share: number }[] = []
    j.topics.forEach((tid, k) => {
      const w = weight.get(tid)
      if (w !== undefined) {
        fit += w * j.shares[k]
        topics.push({ id: tid, share: j.shares[k] })
      }
    })
    if (fit > 0) out.push({ id: j.id, fit, topics })
  }
  out.sort((a, b) => b.fit - a.fit || a.id.localeCompare(b.id))
  return out.slice(0, limit)
}
```

Create `web/src/pages/MatchPage.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useMemo, useRef, useState, type FormEvent, type RefObject } from 'react'
import { JournalLink } from '../components/JournalLink'
import { ModuleFile } from '../components/ModuleFile'
import { PageHeader } from '../components/PageHeader'
import { RequireRelease } from '../components/RequireRelease'
import { Section } from '../components/Section'
import { ShareBar } from '../components/ShareBar'
import { TopicLink } from '../components/TopicLink'
import { useReleaseFile, useTopicDetail } from '../data/atlasContext'
import { hasModule, MODULE_FILES, type AtlasRelease } from '../data/release'
import type { JournalsFile, MatchIndex, Topic } from '../data/types'
import { fmtInt, fmtPct } from '../lib/format'
import { useI18n } from '../lib/i18n'
import { prepareIndex, queryLength, rankJournals, rankTopics, type JournalFit, type TopicHit } from '../lib/matcher'

export default function MatchPage() {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  return (
    <RequireRelease title={t('mt.title')}>
      {(release) => (
        <ModuleFile<MatchIndex> release={release} module="match" path={MODULE_FILES.match} title={t('mt.title')}>
          {(index) => <Match release={release} index={index} />}
        </ModuleFile>
      )}
    </RequireRelease>
  )
}

interface Result {
  hits: TopicHit[]
  journals: JournalFit[]
}

/** The pasted text lives in component state only: never in the URL, storage or a request. */
function Match({ release, index }: { release: AtlasRelease; index: MatchIndex }) {
  const { t } = useI18n()
  const prepared = useMemo(() => prepareIndex(index), [index])
  const [text, setText] = useState('')
  const [result, setResult] = useState<Result | null>(null)
  const [short, setShort] = useState(false)
  const resultsRef = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null)
  const need = index.min_query_chars
  const submit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (queryLength(text) < need) {
      setShort(true)
      setResult(null)
      return
    }
    setShort(false)
    const hits = rankTopics(prepared, text).filter((h) => release.topicById.has(h.id))
    setResult({ hits, journals: rankJournals(index, hits) })
    window.setTimeout(() => resultsRef.current?.focus(), 0)
  }
  return (
    <div className="container">
      <PageHeader title={t('mt.title')} lede={t('mt.lede')}>
        <p className="privacy">{t('mt.privacy')}</p>
      </PageHeader>
      <form className="match-form" onSubmit={submit}>
        <label htmlFor="match-text">{t('mt.label')}</label>
        <textarea
          id="match-text"
          value={text}
          onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)}
          aria-describedby={short ? 'match-short' : undefined}
          autoComplete="off"
          spellCheck={false}
        />
        {short ? <p id="match-short" className="hint" role="alert">{t('mt.tooShort', { n: fmtInt(need), have: fmtInt(queryLength(text)) })}</p> : null}
        <div className="match-actions">
          <button type="submit" className="btn">{t('mt.submit')}</button>
          <button type="button" className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => { setText(''); setResult(null); setShort(false) }}>{t('mt.clear')}</button>
        </div>
      </form>
      {result ? <Results release={release} index={index} result={result} headingRef={resultsRef} /> : null}
    </div>
  )
}

function Results({ release, index, result, headingRef }: { release: AtlasRelease; index: MatchIndex; result: Result; headingRef: RefObject<HTMLHeadingElement | null> }) {
  const { t, lang } = useI18n()
  const top = result.hits[0] ? (release.topicById.get(result.hits[0].id) as Topic) : null
  const jf = useReleaseFile<JournalsFile>(hasModule(release, 'journals') && result.journals.length ? MODULE_FILES.journals : null)
  const byId = new Map((jf.status === 'ready' ? jf.data.journals : []).map((j) => [j.id, j]))
  const topicOf = (id: string) => release.topicById.get(id) as Topic
  return (
    <section className="sec" aria-labelledby="match-results">
      <h2 id="match-results" ref={headingRef} tabIndex={-1}>{t('mt.topics.h')}</h2>
      <p className="hint" role="status">{t('mt.resultsN', { n: fmtInt(result.hits.length) })}</p>
      {result.hits.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="note">{t('mt.none')}</p>
      ) : (
        <>
          <p className="intro">{t('mt.topics.intro')}</p>
          <ol className="hits">
            {result.hits.map((h) => (
              <li key={h.id}>
                <TopicLink topic={topicOf(h.id)} showStatus />
                <ShareBar value={h.score} />
              </li>
            ))}
          </ol>
          {top ? <Works topic={top} /> : null}
          {result.journals.length && jf.status === 'ready' ? (
            <Section id="match-journals" title={t('mt.journals.h')} intro={t('mt.journals.intro', { n: fmtInt(index.fit_topics) })}>
              <ol className="hits">
                {result.journals.filter((j) => byId.has(j.id)).map((j) => (
                  <li key={j.id}>
                    <span><JournalLink journal={byId.get(j.id)!} /></span>
                    <ShareBar value={j.fit} />
                    <span className="topic-sub">
                      {t('mt.fitTopics', { list: j.topics.map((x) => `${lang === 'en' ? topicOf(x.id).en || topicOf(x.id).th : topicOf(x.id).th} ${fmtPct(x.share, 0)}`).join(' · ') })}
                    </span>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ol>
            </Section>
          ) : null}
        </>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}

function Works({ topic }: { topic: Topic }) {
  const { t, name } = useI18n()
  const detail = useTopicDetail(topic.id)
  const arts = detail.status === 'ready' ? detail.detail.articles.slice(0, 5) : []
  if (arts.length === 0) return null
  return (
    <Section id="match-works" title={t('mt.works.h')} intro={t('mt.works.intro', { topic: name(topic) })}>
      <ol className="articles">
        {arts.map((a) => (
          <li key={a.article_id}>
            {a.url ? <a href={a.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">{a.title}<span className="sr-only"> {t('newTab')}</span></a> : <span>{a.title}</span>}
            <span className="art-meta">{[a.journal, a.year ? String(a.year) : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </Section>
  )
}
```

In `web/src/components/Header.tsx` below `  { to: '/changes', key: 'nav.changes' },` insert `  { to: '/match', key: 'nav.match' },`.

## Definition of Done

- Pipeline: `uv run ruff check .`, `uv run pytest -q` and `uv run pytest -m slow -q` green; a release built with `atlas run` lists `journals`, `journal_detail`, `methods`, `match` and (from the second release on) `diff` in `manifest.modules`; `atlas validate` exits 0 on it; `atlas publish --site` keeps `match.json` out of `atlas-offline.html`; no abstract text and no person-level field appears in any published file.
- Config: every new threshold is a key in `AtlasConfig` and documented in `config.example.yaml`; `methods.provider` defaults to `off` and nothing calls an LLM unless it is switched on.
- Web: `npm run lint && npx tsc -b && npm test && npm run build && npm run check:bundle && npm run e2e` green — every new route renders with the example and the empty release, axe 0 serious/critical in light and dark, no horizontal scroll at 360 px, the offline file works (and explains `/match`), the pasted abstract never reaches the URL; the entry chunk ≤ 180 KB gzip and the five editors' pages are lazy chunks.
- Docs: CLAUDE.md, DATA_SPEC, OPERATIONS, ARCHITECTURE, ROADMAP, DESIGN.md and both READMEs describe P2; the owner questions Q1/Q2 are recorded in the roadmap.
- Spec verification "P2+" (precision on hand-labelled sets, rollback drill) is **not** in this plan — the labelled sets do not exist yet; ROADMAP records it as the next quality step.

## Self-review (2026-10-19)

- **Spec coverage:** journals module + registry (T1, T6, T9) · D2 (T2) · D3 incl. foreign authors (T2) · D4 (T3) · D1 scope drift (T3) · D5 special issue (T3) · D7 early: methods per journal (T6, T13) · methods lexicon TH/EN + sample size (T4), LLM optional and off (T5) · E11 monoculture (T4, T14) · E5 quarter diff (T8, T15) · R3 match my abstract, TF-IDF in the browser, no model download (T7, T16) · R4 where to publish (T7, T16) · contract additions + schema regeneration (T9) · validate/publish (T10) · pages `/journals` `/journals/:id` `/methods` `/changes` `/match` + nav + i18n + CSV + a11y (T11–T16) · lazy `/match` and `/journals` (T12–T16) · docs + CI (T17).
- **Placeholders:** none — every code step carries the complete code that was run; generated artifacts (`match_parity.json`, fixtures, schema, `contract.ts`) come from committed scripts.
- **Type consistency:** names used across tasks were checked by running the whole sequence (`journal_code` ids `so02.X` in pipeline, fixtures and e2e; `MODULE_FILES`/`hasModule`/`useReleaseFile` in every page; `JournalsFile` fields `special_issue_*`, `drift_reference_years`, `inhouse_flag_share` in contract, export and pages).
- **Known limits (accepted, recorded in ROADMAP by T17):** the country lexicon is precision-first (Thai text names a foreign country only with "ประเทศ"); the matcher compares words, not meaning (the page says so); the methods lexicon misses methods described in unusual words (coverage is published next to every share).
